| *(this commit)* | *(none — fork-only fix; corrects the M4-05 row's claim)* | **An opencode child session's permission/question prompt now reports under the ROOT session that owns it, instead of under no session at all; `OPENCODE_INTEGRATION_VERSION` 10 -> 11 on BOTH opencode assets.** Codex M4 P2 (`msg_a12f9abba8700337`), consolidated verdict `msg_9f7689e31fce8932` + anchor correction `msg_598a856373689990`; the exact `file:line` anchors below are Codex's and supersede the approximate ranges in this commit's brief. **A corrected M4-05 claim.** The `be901fea` `60c04005` `f23e3779` row above closes with "the child-prompt path deliberately omits `agent_session_id` precisely so the stored session triple and the owner-coherence check keep the root session". That is upstream's intent, but it is **wrong for this fork's Rust side**. The asset's child branch at `src/integration/assets/opencode/zynk-agent-state.js:139-146` (at `d1ef977`) stores only the child id, DROPS the `parentID` it was just handed, and calls `reportState(state)` with no session at all. `set_hook_authority_with_custom_status_at` then takes that missing `session_ref` as the owner's session outright: `src/terminal/state.rs:536-544` clears `persisted_agent_session` and installs a `HookAuthority` whose `session_ref` is `None`, after which `owned_session_ref` (`:770`) returns `None`. A session-less full-lifecycle report therefore REPLACES the pane's session anchor rather than leaving it alone. Two consequences, both real: (a) a child `permission.asked` under the pane's OWN root erases that root's session anchor, so a message addressed to it just before the prompt can no longer be receipted — `zynk.message_received` answers `receiver_identity_mismatch`; (b) a child of ANOTHER attached client's root bypasses `opencode_state_report_is_cross_talk`, whose whole comparison is `owned_session_ref(...).zip(session_ref.as_ref()).is_some_and(|(anchored, incoming)| &anchored != incoming)` (`src/terminal/state.rs:1105-1107`) — `zip` yields `None` when either side is absent, so a session-less report can never compare unequal and is never dropped; the pane flips Idle -> Blocked, undoing exactly what the `ccccda54` row above installed. **Why M4-05's own proof missed it:** that row's runtime proof drove the installed asset against a real socket and asserted on the SIX REQUESTS THE ASSET EMITTED — "a child `permission.asked` `blocked` and a child `question.replied` `working` both with no `agent_session_id`" — a faithful reading of the wire that says nothing about what the server then did to the pane's stored session. Codex caught it by carrying the same asset one step further: import the real `src/integration/assets/opencode/zynk-agent-state.js` under `bun -e` against an isolated server socket, then read `pane.get` and attempt the receipt. **Fix (asset-side only).** The `childSessions` Set becomes a `childParents` Map (child id -> parent id), filled at the same `info?.id && info.parentID` site that previously discarded the parent; new `rootSessionIDFor(sessionID)` walks the parent links upward behind a `visited` Set and returns the topmost KNOWN ancestor (the first id that is not itself a tracked child), or `undefined` on a cycle; the child branch resolves that root and reports `await reportState(state, root)`, sending NOTHING when the root is unresolvable — never a session-less report again. An ancestry that runs past what this process has seen yields that ancestor as-is, which costs at most a report the `:1105-1107` guard filters, never a stolen anchor. `chat.message`'s child guard moves from `.has` on the Set to `.has` on the Map (same predicate). **The Rust side is deliberately NOT changed:** a session-less full-lifecycle report still replaces the anchor; that is the contract, and the asset now respects it. Every other asset path is byte-identical — the `requestChain` serialization, `SESSION_STATE_BY_STATUS`, the root switch, and the `reportedRootSessionID` bookkeeping (`reportState` still records whatever session it reported under, so a child report of a foreign root now updates it exactly as a root-level foreign `session.status` already did; the Rust guard, not this variable, is the filter). **Observed and deliberately NOT changed, for the reviewer:** `session.error` arrives server-global without a `sessionID`, so its root-level `reportState("blocked", undefined)` hits the identical `:536-544` contract and clears the anchor the same way — outside this P2's scope, left as it stands. The `childParents` Map, like the Set before it, is never pruned on `session.deleted` (upstream behaviour, unchanged). **Version parity:** `OPENCODE_INTEGRATION_VERSION` 10 -> 11 (`src/integration/mod.rs:129`) with the `// ZYNK_INTEGRATION_VERSION=` marker moved in BOTH `zynk-agent-state.js` and `zynk-tui-session.js`, which `bundled_integration_asset_versions_match_expected_versions` enforces in both directions. As the M4-05 row already recorded, `src/integration/tests.rs` needs no edit: its two opencode rows (`:2914`, `:2921`) and `opencode_status_requires_the_tui_plugin_and_config_entry` (`:2406`) all read the const, and a grep across `src/` and `tests/` for a hardcoded opencode version marker now matches nothing. **`NOTICE` unchanged:** every touched file that carries post-`cd5ea1be` upstream code — `src/integration/assets/opencode/zynk-agent-state.js`, its `.test.ts`, `zynk-tui-session.js`, `src/integration/mod.rs` and `tests/zynk_receipt.rs` — is ALREADY in the `<!-- apache-modified-files -->` list (`NOTICE:71-74`, `:82`, `:93`), so there is nothing to append. **Tests, RED first.** Bun (`zynk-agent-state.test.ts`, 7 -> 10 tests): `reports child prompts without replacing the root session` is renamed `reports child prompts under the owning root session` and its five `agent_session_id` expectations move `undefined` -> `"root-session"`; three new tests cover a grandchild (`reports a nested child's prompt under its root`), a foreign root (`does not attribute another root's child prompt to the last reported root` — the request must carry `other-root`, not the `root-session` the pane last reported), and a cycle (`sends nothing for a child whose ancestry is cyclic` — zero requests). On `d1ef977` all four fail (6 pass, 4 fail): the five/one/one session ids read `undefined` where `root-session`/`root-session`/`other-root` is expected, and the cyclic child emits one `pane.report_agent` `blocked` where none is allowed. Rust end-to-end (`tests/zynk_receipt.rs`, +3): `opencode_child_prompt_through_the_real_asset(parent, nested)` transcribes Codex's probe without its `review_` prefix. It anchors a detected opencode pane on `visible-root`, then — the control that makes the RED meaningful — sends a first message and asserts its receipt is already `received` ("initial root receipt must succeed"), proving the anchor was VALID before the replay; only then does it submit the second message, drive the REAL bundled asset under `bun -e` (`ZYNK_ENV=1`, `ZYNK_PANE_ID`, `ZYNK_SOCKET_PATH`) so a child of `parent` asks a permission, and return `pane.get` plus the second receipt. Both the own-root and the foreign-root tests keep that control. `an_own_opencode_child_prompt_keeps_the_root_receipt_anchor` and `a_nested_own_opencode_child_prompt_keeps_the_root_receipt_anchor` assert the pane reads `blocked` AND the receipt is `received`; `another_clients_opencode_child_prompt_leaves_the_pane_alone` asserts the pane stays `idle`. All three fail on `d1ef977` — the two own-root probes with `receiver_identity_mismatch` (`left: Null`, `right: "received"`), the foreign one with `left: Some(String("blocked"))`, `right: Some(String("idle"))`. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=~/workspace/.zynk-verify-target` + isolated `ZYNK_HOME`/`ZYNK_SQLITE_HOME`/`ZYNK_SOCKET_PATH`/`XDG_CONFIG_HOME` and a short `TMPDIR` for the SUN_LEN budget):** `cargo fmt --check` and `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` clean; `just test-ts` 35/35 across four files (up from 32); `cargo nextest run --locked --bin zynk integration::` 157/157 (unchanged); `--bin zynk -E 'test(/terminal::state::tests::/)'` 124/124; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52 twice (49 -> 52); the full `--bin zynk` suite 2701/2701 passed, 1 skipped (unchanged — this commit adds no Rust unit test); `python3 -m unittest scripts.test_agent_detection_manifest_check scripts.test_conventional_commits` 12/12; `just gate` clean. |

## M4 closure note (Codex Gate-2 APPROVE at `38e039b`, `msg_97d75b0fe34b71a0`)

Three facts survive M4's approval. **(1) No-amend-rule deviation, recorded not repeated:** M4-17 was first
committed as `517ac9e` and amended to `38e039b` before any review packet named it; the amend changed one
sentence of a ledger row and nothing else — `git diff --quiet 517ac9e 38e039b -- src tests` passes, so `src/`
and `tests/` are byte-identical across the two and the only delta is `docs/zynk/fork-patch-ledger.md`
(1 insertion, 1 deletion). Every follow-up from here is a NEW commit. **(2) OPEN, nonblocking, carried to B2:**
an opencode `session.error` that arrives without a `sessionID`
(`src/integration/assets/opencode/zynk-agent-state.js:207-208`) still reaches `reportState("blocked",
sessionID)` with a falsy id, and `reportState` (`:132-138`) then omits `agent_session_id` altogether — an
unanchored root-level report. It is pre-existing at `d03c717` and the Gate-2 approval did not fix it.
**(3) Precision to apply in the next opencode-asset pass:** the asset comment at `:112-117` overstates its own
guarantee. `rootSessionIDFor` returns the first ancestor that is not itself a tracked child, returned as-is
when the chain runs past what the process has seen, and only a cycle returns `undefined` and is suppressed;
the "costs a filtered report rather than a stolen anchor" claim therefore rests on the server-side cross-talk
guard at `src/terminal/state.rs:1100-1107`, which drops a mismatched incoming ref ONLY when the pane has
already detected OpenCode (`process_present`) and holds an `owned_session_ref` to compare against. Absent
either, the guard returns `false` and drops nothing — it is a conditional filter, not an unconditional
fail-closed guarantee.

# LINUX-ONLY + v0.8.2 PORT LEDGER — M5 runtime-authority refactor, app state and UI

M5 begins here. The upstream `v0.7.1..v0.8.2` runtime-authority chain routes every TUI mutation through
the same socket handlers the API calls, so a keypress and a `zynk` command take one code path. The chain
`1a4e94e5 -> 8b74fb8a -> 97f78226 -> 9a91a2ac -> 0bab0157 -> edd08e89 -> c97e0986 -> 89fe8975` is
reviewed as ONE unit ending at `89fe8975`; a row below on its own is only the seam, not the finished
behaviour. The licensing split of the M2 section above still applies: an upstream commit dated before
`cd5ea1be` (2026-07-22) arrives under AGPL-3.0-or-later, a later one under Apache-2.0, and only the
latter appends its touched files to the `NOTICE` §4(b) *Modified files* list.

| zynk | upstream | what / where / why / adaptations |
|---|---|---|
| *(this commit)* | `1a4e94e5` *(pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Feature (authority seam): the TUI stops mutating `AppState` inline and dispatches the same `Method` the socket does, so keyboard, mouse and API share one mutation path.** Mechanism: two new App entry points (`src/app/api.rs:28` `dispatch_tui_api_request` → `handle_api_request`; `:39` `dispatch_tui_deferred_api_request` → `handle_deferred_worktree_api_request` over a local `mpsc` channel, `try_recv` for the immediate rejection only), four new socket methods, and rewritten input paths that call handlers instead of `AppState` methods. **Wire contract (ADDITIVE only, no existing `serde(rename)` touched):** `workspace.move` (`src/api/schema.rs:75`), `tab.move` (`:97`), `layout.set_split_ratio` (`:133`), `pane.focus` (`:149`) — params `WorkspaceMoveParams` (`src/api/schema/workspaces.rs:22`), `TabMoveParams` (`src/api/schema/tabs.rs:30`), `LayoutSetSplitRatioParams` (`src/api/schema/panes.rs:128`, beside `LayoutApplyParams` as the fork's module split dictates), `pane.focus` reuses `PaneTarget`. All four are compiler-forced into `api_method_name` (`src/api/server.rs:289,300,318,326`), the App dispatch (`src/app/api.rs:853,887,907,919`) and `request_changes_ui` (`src/api/mod.rs:46,54,57,67`). **Handlers:** `handle_workspace_move` (`src/app/api/workspaces.rs:120`, `workspace_move_failed` on out-of-bounds `insert_index`), `handle_tab_move` (`src/app/api/tabs.rs:172`, `tab_move_failed`, refreshes the tab bar only when the target workspace is active), `handle_layout_set_split_ratio` (`src/app/api/layouts.rs:217`, `invalid_ratio` on a non-finite ratio, `split_not_found` when the path names no split), `handle_pane_focus` (`src/app/api/panes.rs:154`, focuses across tabs AND workspaces then forces `Mode::Terminal`). `TileLayout::set_ratio_at` now returns `bool` (`src/layout.rs:209`) so the handler can distinguish "no such split" from "set" — the recursive `set_ratio_at` (`:580`) returns `false` on a non-`Split` node. **Modal parity in the deferred worktree path** (`src/app/api/worktrees/deferred.rs:377,439,513,571`): a create/remove that fails now restores the TUI modal (`creating`/`removing` cleared, `error` set, `dirty_worktree_requires_force` promoted to `force_confirmation` once), and one that succeeds closes it — the modal is no longer left spinning when the git work is done off the main loop. **Input rewrites:** `src/app/input/mod.rs` routes rename/resize/confirm-close/context-menu keys to new `*_via_api` App methods and pre-focuses the pressed pane through `pane.focus` for intentional left/middle presses in `Terminal`/`Resize`; `AppState::handle_mouse` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs:96`) now returns the new `MouseAction` enum (`:29`) instead of `Option<SettingsAction>` so focus/move/split-ratio decisions leave `AppState` un-mutated and the App performs them via the socket, with `handle_mobile_mouse` (`:1081`) returning the new `MobileMouseResult` three-way (`Ignored`/`Consumed`/`Action`) because a mobile tap must be able to carry an action out; `AppState::focus_pane` (`:1394`) becomes a no-op since focus is now the API's job; `src/app/input/modal.rs:903-1261` adds the `impl App` block (`handle_rename_key_via_api`, `save_rename_modal_via_api`, `handle_resize_key_via_api`, `handle_confirm_close_key_via_api`, `handle_context_menu_key_via_api`, `apply_context_menu_action_via_api`) plus the free `cancel_rename_modal`; `src/app/input/navigate.rs:102-686` adds `execute_tui_navigate_action` and the sixteen `*_via_api` helpers plus `navigate_reserved_action_for_key` and `api_pane_direction`; `src/app/input/terminal.rs:48` and the two `handle_prefix_key`/`handle_navigate_key` call sites now use `execute_tui_navigate_action`; `src/app/mod.rs:858,873,898,917,923,929` turn the `request_new_workspace` / `request_new_tab` / `request_new_workspace_cwd` / `request_submit_worktree_*` flags into `workspace.create` / `tab.create` / `worktree.create` / `worktree.open` / `worktree.remove` dispatches; `src/app/worktrees.rs:543,662,697,881` add `submit_worktree_create_via_api` / `submit_worktree_open_via_api` / `submit_worktree_remove_via_api` plus the free `immediate_api_error_message`. Three `AppState` items gain `pub(crate)` for the new callers (`src/app/actions.rs:260,1303,1474`: `pane_focus_target_indices`, `ensure_agent_panel_entry_visible`, `refresh_tab_bar_view`) and `AppState::move_tab` is DELETED — its only caller was the mouse drag-end that now dispatches `tab.move` (`Workspace::move_tab` stays). Upstream's `handle_navigate_reserved_key` behaviour change is carried as-is: the new predicate returns `None` for `workspace_up`/`workspace_down` so those keys no longer move the sidebar selection from the reserved path (the old free function stays for the AppState-only test harness). **Adaptations vs upstream:** (1) every upstream `env: Default::default()` on `WorkspaceCreateParams` / `TabCreateParams` / `PaneSplitParams` is DROPPED — those structs have no `env` field in this fork (`src/api/schema/{workspaces,tabs,panes}.rs`), which is a v0.8.2 field the fork has not ported; (2) the `#[cfg(windows)]` dirty-checkout pre-check in `submit_worktree_remove_via_api` is DROPPED per ADR 0013 (Linux-only, no `#[cfg(target_os)]` branches) — consistent with the fork already having dropped the same block from `start_api_worktree_remove`; (3) the fork's `SettingsAction` set differs (`SaveHeaderVerbose`/`SaveHeaderMaxWidth`, no `SaveSwitchAsciiInputSourceInPrefix`) so the `MouseAction::Settings` arm mirrors the fork's variants (`src/app/input/mod.rs:282`); (4) upstream's remove-success modal reset sits after `emit_worktree_removed_event`, which this fork never ported (no such `EventKind`), so it lands immediately before the success response is encoded (`src/app/api/worktrees/deferred.rs:571`); (5) upstream's `src/api/schema/tests.rs` does not exist here — its `authority_mutation_requests_round_trip` is retargeted into `src/api/schema.rs` `mod tests`; (6) the identity/rebrand renames in the ported test bodies (`herdr` → `zynk` repo paths and labels). **Invariants checked:** `AppState`/`PaneState` stay pure data — `src/app/state.rs` and `src/pane/state.rs` are untouched and `AppState::test_new()` still has 95 call sites across 15 files that compile (a leaked channel would break all 95); `src/ui.rs` is untouched, so `render(&AppState, frame)` gained no mutation; `src/app/creation.rs` is untouched, so `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (32 lines, sha256 `9430e4c6ec415e59…`); the `AppEvent::HookStateReported` identity region in `src/app/actions.rs` is byte-identical (81-line window, sha256 `dca15a8882ce3742…`); fork-owned `src/app/api/zynk.rs` and the CLI send path (`src/cli/{pane,agent,native}.rs`) are untouched and `zynk.message_received` still routes; the ordered `Method` wire-id list changed by exactly four insertions and no rename. **Tests (RED first):** the new fork-only characterization test `method_wire_ids_are_stable` (`src/api/schema.rs:790`) pins the FULL ordered `Method` → wire-id contract (all 80 ids, including the four new ones and fork-owned `zynk.message_received`) against the list `serde` itself reports for an unknown tag, so a rename, reorder, addition or removal fails the test; `authority_mutation_method_wire_ids_are_pinned` (`:802`) names the four new variants by path so a removal is a compile error; `authority_mutation_requests_round_trip` (`:855`) mirrors upstream's round trip. RED at `38e039b`: 14 compile errors (`error[E0599]` × 8 for the four missing `Method` variants, `error[E0422]` × 6 for `WorkspaceMoveParams`/`TabMoveParams`/`LayoutSetSplitRatioParams`), `cargo test --no-run` exit 101. Plus eight ported handler tests: `layout_set_split_ratio_{updates_existing_split,rejects_missing_split}`, `api_pane_focus_{focuses_direct_target_across_tabs_and_workspaces,rejects_invalid_pane_id}`, `api_tab_move_reorders_tabs_in_target_workspace`, `api_workspace_move_reorders_workspaces`, `submit_worktree_{create,remove}_via_api_restores_modal_on_immediate_rejection`. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0 (no orphaned dead code — `AppState::move_tab` was deleted rather than allowed); `--bin zynk -E 'test(/api::schema/)'` 30/30 (27 -> 30), `-E 'test(/zynk::header/)'` 28/28, `-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `-E 'test(/protocol::wire/)'` 47/47, `-E 'test(/app::/)'` 603/603, the delivery-transition + body-purity set (`delivery_transition_validation_allows_only_m2_transitions`, `delivery_transition_validation_covers_m3a_received_matrix`, `failed_outcome_claims_no_delivery`, `zynk::message`, `zynk::persistence`) 51/51; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2712/2712 passed, 1 skipped (2701 -> 2712, +11 = the eleven new tests); per-file `#[test]`+`#[tokio::test]` counts show no decrease and `src/` totals 2702 -> 2713; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1693 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). |
| *(this commit)* | `8b74fb8a` *(pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Feature (placement events): a workspace or tab that actually moves now emits a `workspace.moved` / `tab.moved` event carrying the reordered list, so a socket client no longer has to re-poll `workspace.list` / `tab.list` after a drag or a `move` command.** Second link of the M5 chain, on top of the `1a4e94e5` seam: the TUI drag now routes through `workspace.move` / `tab.move`, so the event fires for keyboard, mouse and API alike. **Wire contract (ADDITIVE only, no existing `serde(rename)` touched):** two new `EventKind`s — `WorkspaceMoved` (`src/api/schema/events.rs:182`, dot name `workspace.moved` at `:206`) and `TabMoved` (`:187`, `tab.moved` at `:211`) — with the matching `Subscription::WorkspaceMoved` (`:25`) / `Subscription::TabMoved` (`:39`), `EventMatch::WorkspaceMoved` (`:115`) / `EventMatch::TabMoved` (`:135`), `EventData::WorkspaceMoved { workspace_id, insert_index, workspaces }` (`:325`) / `EventData::TabMoved { tab_id, workspace_id, insert_index, tabs }` (`:345`), both added to `PLUGIN_HOOK_EVENT_KINDS` (`:233`, `:238`) so plugins can hook them, and both wired into `ActiveSubscription::new` (`src/api/subscriptions.rs:123`, `:151`). One new response variant `ResponseResult::LayoutSplitRatioSet { layout }` (`src/api/schema/response.rs:148`): `layout.set_split_ratio` stops borrowing `layout_apply`'s response type and answers with its own (`src/app/api/layouts.rs:246`). The `Method` wire-id list is UNCHANGED — this commit adds no method. **`PROTOCOL_VERSION` 14 -> 15** (`src/protocol/wire.rs:16`); the fork's stale ordering test is renamed to match (`client_message_wire_tags_preserve_protocol_15_order`, `:858`), keeping its tag-ordering assertion, and `tests/support/mod.rs:18` gains `pub const CURRENT_PROTOCOL: u32 = 15` so the client-handshake integration tests read the version from one place instead of eleven literals. **`PROTOCOL_VERSION` policy for the rest of the program (port sheet §7.2):** upstream bumps the constant at `8b74fb8a` (15, here), `2bc1724c` (16), `e0758c32` (17), `e7fc85bf` (18), `b76adc15` (19) and `6f311498` (20). `2bc1724c` is EXCLUDED from the port and is never taken, so the fork treats `PROTOCOL_VERSION` as MONOTONE — the max value reached by the commits actually ported, not a replay of every upstream bump. M5 therefore goes 14 -> 15 here and -> 19 at M5-24 (`b76adc15`), and **M8 must DROP the 17 and 18 bumps** (`e0758c32`, `e7fc85bf` — both already below the value M5 leaves behind) **and apply only `6f311498`'s 20**. **Handlers:** `handle_workspace_move` (`src/app/api/workspaces.rs:114`) captures the public id BEFORE the move, emits `WorkspaceMoved` (`:139`) only when the state actually reordered, and answers from the same `workspaces` vector it published; `handle_tab_move` (`src/app/api/tabs.rs:170`) does the same for `TabMoved` (`:203`); `AppState::move_workspace` (`src/app/actions.rs:1188`) now returns `bool` and short-circuits a no-op move (`source_idx == target_idx`) BEFORE `mark_session_dirty`, so a drag that lands where it started neither dirties the session nor emits an event — upstream's `.min(self.workspaces.len())` clamp is dropped because the pre-checked `insert_idx <= len` already bounds `target_idx`. Two private helpers remove the duplicated list building: `App::workspace_list_info` (`src/app/api/workspaces.rs:170`) and `App::tab_list_info` (`src/app/api/tabs.rs:256`), each now serving both the `list` handler and the `move` handler. `plugin_context_for_event` (`src/app/api/plugins/context.rs:57`, `:80`) routes the two new `EventData` variants onto the existing workspace/tab context arms — an exhaustive match, so the compiler forced this. **Adaptations vs upstream:** (1) upstream's `src/api/schema/tests.rs` does not exist here — its event and subscription round trips are retargeted into `src/api/schema.rs` `mod tests`, following M5-01; (2) upstream folds the `LayoutSplitRatioSet` response round trip into `layout_export_apply_round_trip`, which this fork does not have, so it lands as a dedicated `layout_split_ratio_set_response_round_trips` (`src/api/schema.rs:534`); (3) upstream's `KNOWN_EVENT_KINDS` const does not exist in this fork, so its two `+` lines have no target — `PLUGIN_HOOK_EVENT_KINDS` is the fork's only event-kind list and both new kinds are added there; (4) upstream's `EventKind` carries `WorktreeCreated`/`WorktreeOpened`/`WorktreeRemoved`, which this fork never ported, so the fork's list runs 17 -> 19 where upstream's runs 20 -> 22; (5) `tests/cli_wrapper.rs` is kept MINIMAL (six `14` -> `15` literals, no `CURRENT_PROTOCOL` import) because upstream deletes that file in a later milestone; (6) the identity renames in the ported test bodies (`register_spawned_zynk_pid`, `cleanup_spawned_zynk`, `.zynk-test-owner-pid`). **Invariants checked:** the ordered `Method` wire-id list and `METHOD_WIRE_IDS` are byte-identical — this commit changes no method id and `zynk.message_received` still routes; there are no `zynk.*` `EventKind`s to preserve (the conversation layer rides `Method`, not events); fork-owned `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**`, `src/cli/**` and `src/cli.rs` are untouched; `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs` and `src/ui.rs` are untouched, so `AppState`/`PaneState` stay pure data and `render(&AppState, frame)` gained no mutation; `src/app/creation.rs` is untouched, so `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (32 lines, sha256 `9430e4c6ec415e59…`); the `AppEvent::HookStateReported` identity region in `src/app/actions.rs` is byte-identical against `e43db7b` (81-line window, sha256 `145b9ecd18ed1b50…`) — the whole `actions.rs` diff is confined to `move_workspace`. **Tests (RED first):** the new fork-only characterization test `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` (`src/api/schema.rs:927`) pins the FULL ordered `EventKind` contract — all 19 variants as `(variant, serde id, dot name)` triples in `EVENT_KIND_WIRE_IDS` (`:851`) — against the id list `serde` itself reports for an unknown tag, so a rename, reorder, addition or removal fails it; it also asserts `PLUGIN_HOOK_EVENT_KINDS` covers every kind except `pane.output_changed`, so a new event kind cannot silently skip the plugin surface. RED at `e43db7b`: 2 compile errors (`error[E0599]` for the missing `EventKind::WorkspaceMoved` and `EventKind::TabMoved`), `cargo test --no-run` exit 101. Plus the mirrored schema tests — `event_envelope_round_trips` (`:406`) now round trips all three envelopes, `layout_split_ratio_set_response_round_trips` (`:534`) asserts the `"type":"layout_split_ratio_set"` tag, `authority_mutation_requests_round_trip` (`:1024`) gains the `workspace.moved` + `tab.moved` subscribe round trip — and the ported handler tests `api_tab_move_reorders_tabs_in_target_workspace` / `api_workspace_move_reorders_workspaces` (both now assert the emitted event off a shared `EventHub`) and the new `api_workspace_move_noop_does_not_emit_event` (`src/app/api/workspaces.rs:273`). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0; `--bin zynk -E 'test(/api::schema/)'` 32/32 (30 -> 32), `-E 'test(/protocol::wire/)'` 47/47 under the renamed test, `-E 'test(/zynk::header/)'` 28/28, `-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `-E 'test(/app::/)'` 604/604 (603 -> 604), the delivery-transition + body-purity set 51/51; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; the adapted integration suites `--test api_ping --test client_mode --test cross_area --test detach_reattach --test multi_client --test server_headless --test cli_wrapper` 154/154 passed, 1 skipped; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2715/2715 passed, 1 skipped (2712 -> 2715, +3 = the three new tests); per-file `#[test]`+`#[tokio::test]` counts show no decrease and `src/` totals 2713 -> 2716; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1693 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). |
| *(this commit)* | `97f78226` `9a91a2ac` *(both pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Refactor (single mutation path): every TUI modal — rename, resize, confirm-close, context menu, the three worktree dialogs and the tab-bar wheel — now reaches state through the same API handler a socket client calls, and every such call goes through one named dispatch seam.** Third and fourth links of the M5 chain, on top of the `1a4e94e5` seam and the `8b74fb8a` placement events. **`97f78226` (route modal mutations through API authority):** key handling in `src/app/mod.rs:1597` (`route_client_input`) stops calling the free `input::handle_rename_key` / `handle_resize_key` / `handle_confirm_close_key` / `handle_context_menu_key` and calls the `App` methods `handle_rename_key_via_api` / `handle_resize_key_via_api` / `handle_confirm_close_key_via_api` / `handle_context_menu_key_via_api` instead. Mouse handling stops mutating inline: `AppState::handle_mouse` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs`) gains three `MouseAction` variants — `RenameModal(ModalAction)` (`:54`), `ConfirmCloseAccept` (`:55`), `ContextMenu { menu, idx }` (`:56`) — and returns them (`:243`, `:406`, `:413`) instead of calling `apply_rename_action` / `confirm_close_accept` / `apply_context_menu_action`, so the App layer at `src/app/input/mod.rs:323` dispatches them onto `apply_rename_mouse_action_via_api` / `confirm_close_accept_via_api` / `apply_context_menu_action_via_api`. The tab-bar wheel (`src/app/input/mouse.rs:905`) stops calling `AppState::previous_tab` / `next_tab` and computes the wrapped target index, returning `MouseAction::FocusTab` so a scroll emits `tab.focused` like every other focus change. Two new adapters in `src/app/input/modal.rs` remove the duplicated bodies: `apply_rename_mouse_action_via_api` (`:1013`) is now the one place the keyboard and the mouse turn a `ModalAction` into a `workspace.rename` / `tab.create` / `tab.rename` / `pane.rename` dispatch, and `confirm_close_accept_via_api` (`:1025`) is the one place a confirm-close accept becomes `workspace.close`; `handle_rename_key_via_api` (`:917`) and `handle_confirm_close_key_via_api` (`:1071`) both call them. `handle_rename_key` splits: the text-editing half becomes the live `handle_rename_edit_key` (`:594`) that both paths share, while the action half stays as the test-only `handle_rename_key`. Consequently the free direct-mutation functions have no production caller left and are gated `#[cfg(test)]` as characterization targets — `apply_rename_action`, `handle_rename_key`, `handle_resize_key`, `confirm_close_accept`, `handle_confirm_close_key`, `apply_context_menu_action`, `handle_context_menu_key` (`src/app/input/modal.rs`), `AppState::resize_pane` (`src/app/actions.rs:1587`), and in `src/app/worktrees.rs` `open_selected_existing_worktree`, `mark_opened_existing_worktree_membership`, `start_worktree_add`, `start_worktree_remove`; `src/app/input/mod.rs:46` drops the four names from its `pub(crate) use`, and the `Direction` (`src/app/input/modal.rs:3`) and `AppEvent` (`src/app/worktrees.rs:11`) imports become `#[cfg(test)]` with them. The three worktree dialogs submit through the API on Enter: `handle_worktree_create_key` (`:250`), `handle_worktree_open_key` (`:329`) and `handle_worktree_remove_key` (`:612`) now call `submit_worktree_create_via_api` / `submit_worktree_open_via_api` / `submit_worktree_remove_via_api`, and the headless deferred-submit block (`src/server/headless.rs:498`, `:506`, `:514`) is renamed onto the same three, which is the whole of that file's diff. **`9a91a2ac` (route TUI mutations through runtime dispatch):** the new fork-owned-adjacent module `src/app/runtime_mutations.rs` is the single named seam every later chain commit extends — `dispatch_tui_runtime_mutation` / `dispatch_tui_deferred_runtime_mutation` (thin wrappers over `dispatch_tui_api_request` / `dispatch_tui_deferred_api_request`) plus six typed helpers `runtime_workspace_focus` / `runtime_workspace_close` / `runtime_tab_focus` / `runtime_tab_close` / `runtime_pane_focus` / `runtime_pane_close` that build the `Method` themselves; it is ported VERBATIM apart from the module declaration (`src/app/mod.rs:17`) so the later diffs in the chain apply cleanly. Every TUI mutation call site is renamed onto it: 7 in `src/app/input/modal.rs`, 10 in `src/app/input/navigate.rs` plus 6 replaced outright by the typed helpers, 3 in `src/app/mod.rs` (`run`), 3 in `src/app/worktrees.rs`. The single deliberate hold-out is `tui.server.reload_config` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:328`), which stays on `dispatch_tui_api_request` exactly as upstream leaves it — it is a server command, not a state mutation. **Adaptations vs upstream:** (1) upstream's one-line `src/app/api/worktrees.rs` hunk (`#[cfg(test)]` on `emit_worktree_opened_for_workspace`) has NO TARGET here — this fork never ported the `WorktreeCreated`/`WorktreeOpened`/`WorktreeRemoved` event family (recorded in the `8b74fb8a` row above), so no such function exists and the file is left untouched; (2) for the same reason the new `worktree_open_enter_submits_through_api_path` test asserts the three events this fork actually emits (`WorkspaceCreated`, `TabCreated`, `PaneCreated`) where upstream asserts four; (3) this fork lacks upstream's `app_for_worktree_tests_with_event_hub` / `event_kinds` / `shutdown_test_runtimes` test helpers, so they are added alongside the test that needs them and `app_for_worktree_tests` is re-expressed in terms of the new one; (4) the identity renames in the ported test bodies (`zynk` repo/branch labels, `issue/195`). **Invariants checked:** `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs` and `src/ui.rs` are untouched, so `AppState`/`PaneState` stay pure data and `render(&AppState, frame)` gained no mutation — the new module's own test proves the boundary by driving `runtime_workspace_focus` + `runtime_workspace_close` against an `AppState::test_new()` with zero terminal runtimes; fork-owned `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**`, `src/cli/**` and `src/cli.rs` are untouched; the `Method` wire-id list is unchanged (`method_wire_ids_are_stable` green, same 80 ids) and this commit adds no method and no event kind (`event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` green); the fork's receipt + embedding worker install in `src/server/headless.rs` is intact — `install_db_workers` is still defined (`:3836`) and still called at `:1064`, `:3704` and `:3913`, with the `zynk_receipt_worker` / `zynk_embedding_worker` pause, drain and teardown sites at `:1028`, `:1035`, `:1042`, `:1045`, `:1056`, `:1057` and `:3521` byte-for-byte unchanged; `src/app/creation.rs` is untouched, so `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (32 lines, sha256 `9430e4c6ec415e59…`); the `AppEvent::HookStateReported` identity region of `src/app/actions.rs` is byte-identical (81-line window, sha256 `145b9ecd18ed1b50…`), the whole `actions.rs` diff being the one `#[cfg(test)]` attribute on `resize_pane`. **Tests (RED first):** the new fork-only purity test `runtime_workspace_mutations_drive_pure_state_without_a_pty` (`src/app/runtime_mutations.rs:84`) documents the boundary the chain establishes — RED at `7df7be4` with 2 × `error[E0599]` (`no method named runtime_workspace_focus` / `runtime_workspace_close` found for struct `app::App`), `cargo test --no-run` exit 101, because the dispatch module does not exist. Plus the six mirrored upstream tests, all RED before the re-routing and green after: `clicking_rename_save_submits_workspace_rename_through_api_path` and `clicking_context_menu_close_routes_through_api_path` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs`), `route_client_input_rename_enter_submits_through_api_path` and `route_client_input_context_menu_enter_submits_through_api_path` (`src/app/mod.rs`), the new `worktree_open_enter_submits_through_api_path` and the two rerouted-and-renamed `worktree_create_enter_submits_through_api_path` / `worktree_remove_enter_submits_through_api_path` (`src/app/worktrees.rs`, formerly `submit_worktree_*_via_api_restores_modal_on_immediate_rejection`, now driven through `KeyCode::Enter` instead of calling the submit directly). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0 with no orphaned dead code; `--bin zynk -E 'test(/input::modal/) \| test(/input::navigate/) \| test(/worktrees/)'` 121/121, `-E 'test(/api::schema/)'` 32/32, `-E 'test(/zynk::header/)'` 28/28, `-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `-E 'test(/app::/)'` 610/610 (604 -> 610); `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2721/2721 passed, 1 skipped (2715 -> 2721, +6 = the six new tests); per-file `#[test]`+`#[tokio::test]` counts show no decrease (`app/worktrees.rs` 18 -> 19, `app/mod.rs` 100 -> 102, `input/mouse.rs` 48 -> 50, new `app/runtime_mutations.rs` 0 -> 1, all others unchanged) and `src/` totals 2716 -> 2722; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1693 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). |
| *(this commit)* | `0bab0157` *(pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Refactor (one create path): the headless server's deferred workspace/tab creates stop calling the direct `App::create_*` helpers and dispatch `workspace.create` / `tab.create` through the same API handler a socket client hits, so the last server-side mutation bypass is closed.** Fifth link of the M5 chain, on top of the `9a91a2ac` dispatch seam. **Mechanism:** the eleven inline deferred-request blocks in `run` (`src/server/headless.rs:441`) are extracted whole into `handle_deferred_requests_headless` (`:547`), which returns one `bool` the caller turns into `needs_render` + `needs_full_render`; inside it, `request_new_workspace` and `request_new_tab` now build `Method::WorkspaceCreate` / `Method::TabCreate` (the tab create taking `requested_new_tab_name` as `label`) and `request_new_workspace_cwd` builds `WorkspaceCreate { cwd: Some(..) }`, each through the new `dispatch_headless_runtime_mutation` (`:681`) which pushes an `api::ApiRequestMessage` over a local `mpsc` channel, waits 5 s, and maps a decodable `ErrorResponse` to `Err(ErrorBody)` — so a failure is logged with the API `code`/`message` instead of an `io::Error`, and the cwd failure still falls back to `Mode::Navigate`. `handle_api_request_with_shutdown_check` (`:2578`) becomes a thin wrapper over the new `handle_api_request_with_shutdown_check_inner` (`:2582`), whose `skip_default_workspace_for_request` parameter ORs into the existing `ServerStop`/`ServerLiveHandoff` skip (`:2648`): a server-initiated create IS the workspace creation, so the post-request `ensure_default_workspace()` fallback must not race it or paper over a rejected create. With the headless loop off them, the direct-mutation helpers have no production caller left and are gated `#[cfg(test)]` as characterization targets — `App::create_workspace` (`src/app/creation.rs:60`), `create_workspace_with_events` (`:75`), `create_tab` (`:86`), `create_tab_with_options` (`:131`), the `tracing::error` import that only they use (`:3`), and `crate::logging::tab_created` (`src/logging.rs:271`), whose only caller was `create_tab_with_options`. Now that the headless path shares the seam, the `tui`-scoped names lose the qualifier: `dispatch_tui_api_request` -> `dispatch_api_request` and `dispatch_tui_deferred_api_request` -> `dispatch_deferred_api_request` (`src/app/api.rs:28`, `:39`), `dispatch_tui_runtime_mutation` -> `dispatch_runtime_mutation` and `dispatch_tui_deferred_runtime_mutation` -> `dispatch_deferred_runtime_mutation` (`src/app/runtime_mutations.rs:6`, `:10`), renamed across all 21 call sites (7 in `src/app/input/modal.rs`, 10 in `src/app/input/navigate.rs`, 3 in `src/app/mod.rs`, 3 in `src/app/worktrees.rs`, 6 inside `runtime_mutations.rs` itself). The `9a91a2ac` hold-out ends: `tui.server.reload_config` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:327`) moves from the raw API entry point onto `dispatch_runtime_mutation` like every other TUI action. **Behaviour change carried from upstream:** a deferred `tab.create` with no active workspace is now REJECTED by `handle_tab_create` (`workspace_not_found`) where `App::create_tab` used to silently fall back to creating a whole workspace; the new guard test pins that the rejection is not then masked by `ensure_default_workspace()`. **Adaptations vs upstream:** (1) every upstream `env: Default::default()` on `WorkspaceCreateParams` / `TabCreateParams` is DROPPED — those structs have no `env` field in this fork (`src/api/schema/{workspaces,tabs}.rs`), consistent with the same adaptation recorded on the `1a4e94e5` row; (2) the `+334` `src/server/headless.rs` rewrite is re-anchored BY HAND around the fork's DB-worker install rather than applied mechanically, because a mechanical apply can drop the `install_db_workers` call and still compile (the field is an `Option`), with the only symptom every `zynk.message_received` answering `receipt_worker_unavailable`; (3) one fork-only test is added beyond the upstream two (see below), so this file's diff is `+375` rather than `+334`. **Invariants checked:** the fork's receipt + embedding worker install is intact — `install_db_workers` is still DEFINED at `src/server/headless.rs:3924` and still CALLED from the server startup path `run_server` (`:3792`), from `restore_db_workers_after_failed_handoff` (`:1143`) and from the handoff import server (`:4001`), and the receipt suites prove it live (`-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52); `src/app/creation.rs` gains only the five `#[cfg(test)]` lines, so `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (32 lines, sha256 `9430e4c6ec415e59…` before and after); `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs` and `src/ui.rs` are untouched, so `AppState`/`PaneState` stay pure data (97 `AppState::test_new()` and 343 `Workspace::test_new(` sites still compile) and `render(&AppState, frame)` gained no mutation; `src/app/actions.rs` is untouched, so the `AppEvent::HookStateReported` identity region is trivially byte-identical (sha256 `145b9ecd18ed1b50…`); fork-owned `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**` and `src/cli/**` are untouched (0 changed files); the `Method` wire-id list is unchanged (`method_wire_ids_are_stable` green, same 80 ids) and this commit adds no method and no event kind. **Tests (RED first):** the two mirrored upstream tests `headless_deferred_workspace_create_uses_runtime_events` (`src/server/headless.rs:4199`) and `headless_deferred_named_tab_create_uses_runtime_events` (`:4223`) are PARITY tests, not RED ones — run against the pure extraction with the old `create_workspace()`/`create_tab()` bodies still in place they PASS (2/2), which is the point: the refactor must not change the emitted `WorkspaceCreated` -> `TabCreated` -> `PaneCreated` sequence or the `label` on the tab. The genuinely new production behaviour is the skip flag, so a fork-only guard test covers it: `headless_deferred_create_failure_does_not_seed_a_default_workspace` (`:4265`) connects a full app client, leaves `Mode::Onboarding` for `Navigate` so the fallback is live, fires `request_new_tab` with no workspaces, and asserts the rejected create leaves `workspaces` empty with zero terminal runtimes. RED proven by flipping the new argument to `false`: `panicked at src/server/headless.rs:4291: a rejected deferred create must not fall through to ensure_default_workspace`, green with `true`. Two upstream test helpers land with them — `test_headless_server_with_event_hub` (`:4070`, `test_headless_server` now delegates to it) and `shutdown_test_runtimes` (`:4122`, drains `terminal_runtimes` so a test that spawns a real PTY does not leak it). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0 with no orphaned dead code; `--bin zynk -E 'test(/server::headless/)'` 90/90 (87 -> 90), `-E 'test(/input::modal/) \| test(/input::navigate/) \| test(/worktrees/) \| test(/runtime_mutations/)'` 122/122, `-E 'test(/api::schema/)'` 32/32, `-E 'test(/zynk::header/)'` 28/28, `-E 'test(/zynk::receipt/)'` 29/29, `-E 'test(/app::/)'` 610/610; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test multi_client --test detach_reattach --test client_mode --test api_ping --test cross_area` 88/88; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2724/2724 passed, 1 skipped (2721 -> 2724, +3 = the three new tests); per-file `#[test]`+`#[tokio::test]` counts show no decrease (`server/headless.rs` 87 -> 90, all eight other touched files unchanged) and `src/` totals 2722 -> 2725; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1694 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks); `just check` green end to end — 3042/3042 passed, 2 skipped, plus the 64 maintenance unittests OK. | *(this commit)* | *(none — fork-only test harness fix)* | **Test-only (harness race): the `wait_for_file` test helper waits for the file to be NON-EMPTY, not merely readable, so a poll that lands between an external writer's create and its write no longer returns `""` and fails the caller's content assertion.** No production line changes. **The race:** the fork's `#[cfg(test)] fn wait_for_file` (`src/app/input/mod.rs:657`) polled `std::fs::read_to_string` every 20 ms for up to 2 s and returned the FIRST `Ok(_)` — including `Ok("")`. Its five callers all drive an external writer that creates the destination and then fills it: `terminal_direct_edit_scrollback_opens_editor_pane` (`src/app/input/terminal.rs:852`) and `edit_scrollback_key_opens_focused_runtime_scrollback_in_editor_pane` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:2787`) set `EDITOR` to `sh -c 'cp "$1" <out>' sh`, and `cp` opens the destination with `O_CREAT` + `O_TRUNC` before writing it, so between those two syscalls the path exists with zero length; `direct_custom_command_runs_before_forwarding_to_pane` (`src/app/input/terminal.rs:891`), `custom_command_runs_from_prefix_key_in_navigate_mode` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:2642`) and `pane_overlay_command_opens_and_closes_after_exit` (`:2718`) drive a `printf > <out>` redirect with the same create-then-write shape. A poll landing in that window returned an empty string and `assert!(content.contains("alpha"))` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:2788`) failed. Observed once during M5-03 verification and green on the next 8 runs — a real ordering race, not an environment flake. **The upstream guard the fork dropped:** upstream's helper of the same name and same location, `wait_for_file` in `src/app/input/mod.rs` (v0.8.2 `:900`), wraps the read in `if !content.is_empty() { return content; }` (v0.8.2 `:904`) and has carried it unchanged at every tag from `v0.7.0` through `v0.8.2`. The fork's copy has never had it: `git log --all -S'if !content.is_empty()' -- src/app/input/mod.rs` returns no commit on this branch, so the guard was lost in the initial squashed import (`chore: initial public release`), not by a later edit — it is not an M5 regression. **The fix:** the helper now polls until the file exists AND has non-zero length, under the SAME 2 s deadline and 20 ms interval, and its timeout panic names both the elapsed time and the path (`timed out after {:?} waiting for non-empty {}`) instead of the path alone; a comment records why the guard must not be dropped again. Upstream's extra `#[cfg(unix)]` attribute is deliberately NOT taken back — this fork is Linux-only (ADR 0013). All five callers were audited against the change: every writer emits a non-empty payload (a `cp` of a scrollback holding `alpha\nbeta`, `printf direct`, a three-line `printf`, `printf done`), so none treats an empty file as "ready"; the single discarded result (`let _ = wait_for_file(...)`, `src/app/input/navigate.rs:2718`) uses the call purely as a barrier before the overlay-teardown wait, where blocking until the write completes is strictly stronger. **RED first:** with the pre-fix helper and the editor command temporarily widened to `sh -c 'touch <out>; sleep 0.2; cp "$1" <out>' sh`, so the poll reliably observes the empty file, the target test failed 5/5 — `panicked at src/app/input/navigate.rs:2791: assertion failed: content.contains("alpha")` after 0.171 s, i.e. the helper returned early rather than reaching its 2 s deadline, which is the empty-read path and not a timeout. With the guard applied and that same widened command still in place: 5/5 pass. The temporary widening was then reverted, leaving `src/app/input/navigate.rs` byte-identical to the M5-04 base. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`/`ZYNK_*`/`XDG_*`/`TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0; the target test 20/20 consecutive; under load `--bin zynk --test-threads 32 -E 'test(/app::/)'` 610/610; `-E 'test(/app::input::navigate/)'` 52/52; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2724/2724 passed, 1 skipped; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1694 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). `NOTICE` unchanged — the change is fork-owned test scaffolding with no upstream commit behind it. |
| *(this commit)* | `edd08e89` `c97e0986` `89fe8975` *(all pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Refactor (chain end — typed adapters): the pane-layout, worktree and remaining workspace/tab TUI actions stop hand-rolling `Method` values at the call site and go through typed adapters on `App`, so `src/app/runtime_mutations.rs` is now the ONE place a TUI action becomes an API method.** This row ENDS the chain `1a4e94e5 -> 8b74fb8a -> 97f78226 -> 9a91a2ac -> 0bab0157 -> edd08e89 -> c97e0986 -> 89fe8975` — the three commits are consecutive upstream and the third rewrites what the first two add, so they land as one fork commit and the state here is what the chain is reviewed as. **`edd08e89` (pane layout):** the split/resize/swap/zoom/focus-direction/rename/set-split-ratio call sites in `src/app/input/navigate.rs` and `src/app/input/modal.rs` swap `dispatch_runtime_mutation(id, Method::X(params))` for `runtime_x(id, params)`; `runtime_mutations.rs` gains seven adapters (`runtime_pane_rename`, `runtime_pane_focus_direction`, `runtime_pane_resize`, `runtime_pane_swap`, `runtime_pane_split`, `runtime_pane_zoom`, `runtime_layout_set_split_ratio`). Callers are `src/app/input/navigate.rs` (workspace create/move/focus/close, tab create/move/focus/close, pane focus/focus-direction/swap/split/zoom/close, layout set-split-ratio, server reload-config), `src/app/input/modal.rs` (workspace rename, tab create/rename, pane rename/resize/swap and the two close paths), `src/app/mod.rs` (the three `tick` request flags) and `src/app/worktrees.rs` (the three worktree submits). | *(this commit)* | `2ff5dd2f` `db1ef28d` `14d8e933` `b44ca3b3` `32e3d7b7` `bc764c83` `5fffc59c` *(all pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **UI chrome group: every chrome label is now measured by DISPLAY WIDTH through one oracle (`src/ui/text.rs`), the mobile switcher leads with agents and renders worktrees as a tree, and three small chrome fixes land with it.** Seven upstream commits, applied in chronological order as one fork commit because `b44ca3b3` rewrites the width math `2ff5dd2f` introduces and `5fffc59c` rewrites the border title `2ff5dd2f` touches — a row per commit would describe states that never ship. **`2ff5dd2f` (cjk pane border labels):** `truncate_label` stops counting `chars()` and measures `UnicodeWidthStr::width`, breaking the prefix at a whole-character boundary; `render_pane_border_titles` (`src/ui/panes.rs`) stops writing the title one `char` per cell — a wide glyph used to occupy one cell and shift everything after it — and hands the whole string to `Buffer::set_stringn` with the clip width, which lays wide cells the way the emulator does. **`db1ef28d` (agents-first mobile switcher):** the switcher's agents section moves ABOVE spaces/tabs/menu in all three places that must agree — hit-test (`mobile_switcher_target_at`), height (`mobile_switcher_content_height`) and render (`render_mobile_switcher_content`) — and is omitted entirely when there are no agents, so the new `mobile_agents_block_height` offsets every section below it and `mobile_switcher_workspace_doc_range` gains an `&AppState` parameter (its one caller, `AppState::ensure_mobile_switcher_workspace_visible`, `src/app/actions.rs:1074`, passes `self`). The header's second row swaps the single-state `agent_priority_label` for a GLOBAL roll-up across every workspace: `global_agent_counts` → `agent_summary_segments` (ordered blocked → done → working → idle, collapsing to `all idle` / `no agents`) → `fit_summary_segments` (greedy fit, drops the least urgent tail and reports it) → `agent_summary_line`, with only the leading segment keeping its state colour; a blocked agent anywhere also paints a red `●` badge on the switch button. **`14d8e933` (worktree tree in the switcher):** `workspace_list_entries` splits into `workspace_list_entries_inner(app, force_expanded)` with a new `workspace_list_entries_expanded` that ignores `collapsed_space_keys` — mobile has no collapse affordance — and `grouped_child_display_label` / `next_entry_is_indented_workspace` become `pub(crate)` for the switcher. The switcher's spaces section now iterates that grouped entry list instead of raw `app.workspaces` order in all three places (doc range by POSITION not array index, hit-test mapping the row back to the real `ws_idx`, render), children get `├─ `/`└─ ` connectors with a matching detail-row continuation, and `AppState::visible_workspace_order` (`src/app/actions.rs:1129`) uses the expanded list when `view.layout == ViewLayout::Mobile` so keyboard order matches what is drawn. **`b44ca3b3` (measure ui labels by display width — the highest-value commit here):** NEW `src/ui/text.rs` (`display_width`, `display_width_u16`, `truncate_end`, `middle_elide`, `take_prefix_width`, `take_suffix_width`) becomes the SINGLE chrome-width oracle and four near-duplicate private helpers are deleted — `truncate_text` in `dialogs.rs`, `navigator.rs` and `sidebar.rs`, `truncate` in `mobile.rs`, `middle_elide` in `navigator.rs`, `truncate_label` in `panes.rs` — with every call site rewritten (3 dialogs, 6 mobile, 2 navigator, 2 panes, 3 sidebar). Byte-length and char-count width math goes with them: the toast rect stopped sizing on `title.len()` (BYTES — a CJK toast was ~3× too wide), `tab_width` on `chars().count()` (a CJK tab clipped its last glyph), and the same in `agent_panel_toggle_rect`, the navigator row budget and the dialogs pad. **Boundary (binding):** `src/ui/text.rs` measures with `unicode_width` and is for CHROME only; terminal CELL geometry (`visible_text_cells`, `logical_cell_for_visible_cell`, `last_character_col`, `text_cells`, `char_cell_width`) keeps calling `crate::ghostty::unicode_codepoint_width` / `unicode_grapheme_width`, the emulator's own table — a module doc records it and the fork-only test `chrome_display_width_agrees_with_the_terminal_cell_oracle` pins that the two agree per codepoint. `src/zynk/header.rs`'s own `unicode_width` use is untouched (it is wire-header layout, not `src/ui/` chrome). **`32e3d7b7` (compact indexed keybind help):** `indexed_label` (`src/ui/keybind_help.rs`) scans for CONSECUTIVE 9-runs via the new `indexed_range_prefix` instead of only collapsing when the whole binding list is exactly 9 long, so `switch_tab = ["prefix+1..9", "alt+1..9"]` renders `prefix+1..9 / alt+1..9` instead of eighteen spelled-out keys. **`bc764c83`:** the ACTIVE auto-named tab drops `Modifier::DIM` (dim-on-accent was unreadable); the inactive auto-named tab keeps it, which `ui::tests::tab_bar_dims_auto_named_tabs_and_emphasizes_custom_tabs` still guards. **`5fffc59c`:** the focused pane's `▌` title marker goes away — `pane_border_title` takes `_focused` and both branches collapse to the unfocused form, so a focused label now gets the same 4-column budget (`" abc… "`, not `"▌ ab… "`); focus is still signalled by the accent + BOLD border style. **Adaptations beyond a rename:** (1) the fork had already removed upstream's `mobile_tab_status` helper and inlines `format!("tab {}/{}", …)` in both the header and the switcher detail row — preserved, so `14d8e933`'s `{detail_prefix}` change wraps the fork's string, and `src/ui/mobile.rs`'s production half is otherwise byte-identical to upstream `b44ca3b3`; (2) `b44ca3b3`'s `format_agent_panel_primary_label` hunk has NO fork counterpart — the fork's sidebar rewrite deleted that function — so its intent lands on the fork's own agent-panel row instead: `render_agent_detail` moves BOTH its truncation (`truncate_end`) and its right-align padding (`display_width` for `label_width` and the `spans` sum) onto the same oracle, because leaving the padding on `chars().count()` while the name truncates by width would overflow the row for a CJK agent name; (3) the `SB-001` comment above the branch truncation is retitled from "by CHARACTER (`truncate_text`)" to "by DISPLAY WIDTH (`truncate_end`)" — the panic it guards against (a byte index inside a codepoint) is unchanged; (4) `ToastHerdrPosition` → `ToastZynkPosition` and the CJK test fixtures use `zynk`, which is one column narrower than `herdr`, so `truncate_end("提交 zynk 的反馈", 15)` replaces upstream's `(…"提交 herdr 的反馈", 16)` at the same cut point; (5) the two `chars().count()` calls inside `fit_summary_segments` / `agent_summary_line` are upstream's own and deliberately NOT converted — they measure the ASCII summary segments (`◉ 2 blocked`, `● 1 done`, …) whose glyphs are all one column, so char count and display width are equal there. **Tests (RED first, two waves):** wave A added the seven behaviour tests that compile on the M5-05 base — `pane_border_title_truncates_cjk_by_display_width`, `pane_border_renderer_places_adjacent_cjk_by_display_width`, `tab_width_uses_display_width_for_cjk_labels`, `tab_bar_renders_trailing_cjk_character`, `active_auto_named_tab_keeps_readable_weight`, `toast_rect_uses_display_width_for_cjk_labels`, `keybind_help_compacts_multiple_indexed_ranges` — plus the two rewritten `pane_border_title_trims_and_truncates` assertions, and ran 8 FAILED / 102 passed of 110; wave B then added the tests that CANNOT compile on the base (the `src/ui/text.rs` module and the nine mobile tests, which need `GlobalAgentCounts` / `SummaryTone` / `agent_summary_segments` / `fit_summary_segments` and the new `mobile_switcher_workspace_doc_range(&app, idx)` arity) and failed to build with 30 errors. All 19 pass after the port. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*` / `TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exit 0; `-E 'test(/ui::/) or test(/^ui/)'` 103 → 122; `test(/app::/)` 618/618; `test(/zynk::header/)` 28/28 (unmoved); `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test cross_area --test client_mode` 41/41; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2751/2751 passed, 1 skipped (2732 at the base, +19); `just check` 3069/3069 passed, 2 skipped plus 64 maintenance unittests OK; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1694 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). Per-file `#[test]` counts, base → head: `src/ui.rs` 26 → 27, `src/ui/text.rs` 0 → 3 (new), `src/ui/panes.rs` 15 → 17, `src/ui/mobile.rs` 3 → 12, `src/ui/tabs.rs` 2 → 5, `src/ui/status.rs` 3 → 4; `src/ui/sidebar.rs` 41, `src/ui/dialogs.rs` 3, `src/ui/navigator.rs` 0, `src/ui/keybind_help.rs` 0 and `src/app/actions.rs` 92 unchanged — none dropped; repo-wide 2794 → 2813 `#[test]` with `#[tokio::test]` unchanged at 188. **Invariants:** `src/app/state.rs` and `src/pane/state.rs` untouched — both `*State` types stay pure data; `ui::render` gains NO mutation, and the only three `src/ui.rs` hunks are the `mod text;` declaration, the `sidebar::` re-export list and one test inside `mod tests` (`git diff -U0 -- src/ui.rs` shows exactly `@@ -20,0 +21 @@`, `@@ -75,3 +76,3 @@`, `@@ -1261,0 +1263,35 @@`); `grep -rn 'AtomicBool\|RefCell\|Cell<' src/ui.rs src/ui/` returns only the two pre-existing `AtomicBool` `PaneRuntime::new` arguments inside the `#[cfg(test)]` modules of `mobile.rs` and `sidebar.rs`, both present at the base and unreachable from the draw path; `grep -rn 'UnicodeWidth' src/ui/` returns `src/ui/text.rs` ONLY; `src/ui/panes.rs`, `src/ui/navigator.rs` and `src/ui/keybind_help.rs` are byte-identical to upstream `5fffc59c` / `b44ca3b3` / `32e3d7b7`. `NOTICE` unchanged — all seven commits (2026-06-25 … 2026-06-29) predate the `cd5ea1be` relicense (2026-07-22). |
| *(this commit)* | `f54d8e8c` `2a1a8d64` *(both pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Two config-backed UI features that both buy back terminal rows/columns: a collapsed sidebar can now take ZERO width, and a workspace holding one tab can drop its tab row.** Applied as one fork commit because both are the same shape — a new `[ui]` key that only changes geometry inside `compute_view_internal` — and both land the same test pair. **`f54d8e8c` (hidden collapsed sidebar):** new `SidebarCollapsedModeConfig` (`src/config/model.rs:172`, `#[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]`, `Compact` default) becomes `ui.sidebar_collapsed_mode` (`:844`), is re-exported from `src/config.rs`, and reaches `AppState.sidebar_collapsed_mode` (`src/app/state.rs:1379`, plain `Copy` data) at construction (`src/app/mod.rs:598`) and on reload (`:1312`). Geometry: the collapsed sidebar's width stops being the constant `COLLAPSED_WIDTH` and matches on the mode (`src/ui.rs:222-226`), `Hidden` giving `0` — every rect below it already derives from that one `Layout::horizontal`, so the tab row and terminal area widen with no further change. Rendering gains two zero-size guards: `render_with_runtime_registry` only calls a sidebar renderer when `sidebar_area.width > 0` (`src/ui.rs:416`) and `render_sidebar_collapsed` returns early on a zero-width/height area (`src/ui/sidebar.rs:622`) — the separator column it writes would otherwise index `area.x + width.saturating_sub(1)`, i.e. column `x` of the PANE, on a zero-width rect. No hit-test change was needed: `on_sidebar_toggle` already gates on `rect.width > 0`, and `collapsed_sidebar_toggle_rect` of a zero-width rect is zero-width, so a hidden sidebar has no mouse expand hotspot (`keys.toggle_sidebar` remains the only way back) — the upstream test asserting that is a characterization guard, GREEN before the change, and is kept as the regression lock. **`2a1a8d64` (hide single-tab tab row):** `ui.hide_tab_bar_when_single_tab` (`src/config/model.rs:866`, default false) → `AppState.hide_tab_bar_when_single_tab` (`src/app/state.rs:1396`) at construction (`src/app/mod.rs:612`) and reload (`:1334`). The desktop tab-bar/terminal split moves out of `compute_view_internal` into `desktop_tab_bar_and_terminal_area` (`src/ui.rs:195`) so it can be asked PER WORKSPACE, and the old `has_tabs` boolean is replaced by mapping over the active workspace — same `Rect::default()` fallback when there is none. **The subtle half is background-tab resizing.** `resize_background_tab_panes_to_terminal_area` used to hand ONE terminal area to every background tab of every workspace; that is now wrong on desktop, because a workspace's terminal area depends on ITS OWN tab count, not the active workspace's. It splits: `resize_background_tab_panes_for_desktop` (`src/ui.rs:175`) recomputes the split per workspace from `main_area`, while the mobile path keeps the single-area form under the neutral name `resize_background_tab_panes_to_area` (`:156`). Both are `&AppState` and are called only from `compute_view_internal` / `compute_mobile_view`. **Adaptations:** upstream's `docs/next/CHANGELOG.md` + `configuration.mdx` hunks are dropped (`docs/next/` is not tracked here); the `src/ui/tabs.rs` hunk is a single blank-line deletion and is skipped as a pure no-op — the fork's `render_tab_bar` already opens with the `area.width == 0 \|\| area.height == 0` early return the hidden row relies on. Both keys additionally get the fork's new-key test pair (`src/config/io.rs`, §5.3.4): the key round-trips through `load_live_config_from_str` while a misspelled sibling in the same `[ui]` section is reported with its FULL path (`unknown config key ui.sidebar_collapsed_mod` / `ui.hide_tab_bar_when_single_tabb`) — the M2 `serde_ignored` reporting. `reload_config_updates_hide_tab_bar_when_single_tab` (`src/app/mod.rs`) is a fork addition: upstream wired the key into `reload_config` without a test, and reload is the only path by which the key reaches a running app. Both commits predate `cd5ea1be` (2026-07-22), so they arrive under AGPL-3.0-or-later and `NOTICE` §4(b) is unchanged. `src/app/creation.rs`, `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**`, `src/cli/**` and `src/server/headless.rs` are untouched; `AppState` gains only `Copy`/`bool` data; `render` gains one zero-width branch condition and no mutation. |
| *(this commit)* | `974a4817` `6ea68f9a` `5e2dc406` `4e3970ca` *(all pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Four input fixes, none of them related to each other beyond the layer they live in: a tab closed from the context menu leaves the menu, plain page keys reach a primary-screen pager, copy mode learns `ctrl+b`/`ctrl+f`, and the workspace picker answers the arrow keys again.** Two of the four are regressions the M5 authority-seam chain (`1a4e94e5 -> 89fe8975`, rows above) introduced: the `#[cfg(test)]` state-only mirrors of both paths still do the right thing (`src/app/input/modal.rs:757` `apply_context_menu_action`, `src/app/input/navigate.rs:1091` `handle_navigate_reserved_key`), while the production `App` path that replaced them dropped the behaviour — which is why upstream calls them fixes and why the mirrors could not catch either one. **`974a4817` (leave the context menu after closing a tab):** the `ContextMenuKind::Tab` + `Close` arm (`src/app/input/modal.rs:1166`) dispatched the close and returned with `state.mode` still `Mode::ContextMenu`; `self.context_menu` had already been `take()`n by the mouse handler (`src/app/input/mouse.rs:411`), so the user was left in a mode with no menu to render. Now it calls `leave_modal` (`:376`). **ADAPTED:** upstream's one-line `leave_modal(&mut self.state);` is unconditional because upstream's `close_active_tab_via_api` returns `()`. The fork's wrapper is `close_active_tab_via_api_requires_confirmation` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:379`, from `89fe8975`/M5-05) and returns `true` when it opened the confirm dialog instead of closing — an unconditional `leave_modal` would then immediately close the `Mode::ConfirmClose` it just opened. Guarded as `if !…{ leave_modal(…) }`, mirroring the fork's existing `NavigateAction::CloseTab` call site (`:252`). A second, fork-only test locks that branch: `clicking_tab_context_menu_close_last_tab_in_group_keeps_confirmation_mode` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs`) closes the only tab of a worktree-group parent and asserts `Mode::ConfirmClose` survives — it is GREEN before the change and is the regression lock on the guard, next to the upstream test `clicking_tab_context_menu_close_leaves_context_menu_mode` which was RED. **`6ea68f9a` (forward page keys to primary-screen pagers):** the host-scrollback predicate was `!alternate_screen && !mouse_reporting_enabled()`, which is true for `less -X` and Git's pager — they keep the PRIMARY screen but set DECCKM, so zynk swallowed PageUp/PageDown into its own scrollback and the pager never paged. The predicate moves onto `InputState` as `plain_page_keys_use_host_scrollback` (`src/pane/terminal.rs:100`) and adds `&& !self.application_cursor`; both call sites now ask it — the TUI key path (`src/app/input/terminal.rs:103`) and the terminal-attach page-key path (`src/server/headless.rs:242`). Shell transcripts are unaffected: a plain shell does not set application cursor mode. The +181/-… on `src/server/headless.rs` is almost all test code — upstream folds the three `terminal_attach_page_key_*` tests' duplicated tokio-runtime/scrollback setup into `with_terminal_attach_page_key_runtime` + `apply_terminal_attach_page_up` and adds a fourth case, `terminal_attach_page_key_forwards_when_application_cursor`; the production change in that file is the three-line `is_some_and` above. **Fork-critical:** `install_db_workers` (def `src/server/headless.rs:3924`, calls `:1143`, `:3792`, `:4001`) and the receipt-worker install it performs are byte-identical before and after — verified by diffing a grep+def snapshot — and `zynk::receipt` (29), `zynk_receipt` + `zynk_native_cli` (52) and `server_headless` + `cross_area` + `client_mode` + `multi_client` (53) are green. **`5e2dc406` (copy mode ctrl page navigation):** two arms added ahead of the existing `ctrl+u`/`ctrl+d` half-page arms (`src/app/input/copy_mode.rs:128`) so `ctrl+b`/`ctrl+f` scroll a FULL page (`scroll_copy_mode_page(.., ±1, false)`). This retires the characterization test `copy_mode_ignores_prefix_key`, which asserted the old no-op for `ctrl+b`; upstream replaces it with `copy_mode_ctrl_b_uses_page_up` and adds `copy_mode_ctrl_f_uses_page_down`, so the file's test count still rises (16 -> 17). Ordering matters: this lands BEFORE `c8850a2e`'s copy-mode rewrite, which is a later M5 row. **`4e3970ca` (restore workspace picker keyboard navigation):** `App::handle_navigate_key` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:75`) consulted `navigate_reserved_action_for_key`, which explicitly returns `None` for `keys.navigate_workspace_up` / `navigate_workspace_down` (`:1150`) so they cannot be mistaken for pane focus — and nothing downstream handled them, so Up/Down in `Mode::Navigate` did nothing at all. The two `matches_direct_key` checks now run FIRST (`:86-103`), calling `move_selected_workspace_by_visible_delta` (`src/app/actions.rs:1154`) exactly as the `#[cfg(test)]` mirror does. **Adaptations beyond the one above:** upstream's `docs/next/CHANGELOG.md` and `docs/next/website/.../keyboard.mdx` hunks are dropped (`docs/next/` is not tracked here) and no tracked doc enumerates the copy-mode motions — `README.md:188` names only `v`/`y`/`q`. All four commits predate `cd5ea1be` (2026-07-22), so they arrive under AGPL-3.0-or-later and `NOTICE` §4(b) is unchanged. `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs`, `src/ui.rs`, `src/app/creation.rs`, `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**` and `src/cli/**` are untouched; no `AppState`/`PaneState` field is added, `render` is not touched, and `method_wire_ids_are_stable` / `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` are green. |
| *(this commit)* | `4f22e9a7` `d6285aaa` *(pre-relicense)* `58e812b2` *(**post-relicense** — `NOTICE` entries)* | **A ctrl-clicked URL now survives a soft wrap, the render path still never scans for one, and the click's own release stops leaking into the pane.** Three commits, one fork commit, applied in upstream order because the middle one prunes what the first generalises: `4f22e9a7` (2026-07-06) taught the render path to synthesize hyperlink metadata for plain URLs, `d6285aaa` (2026-07-08) took that back out two days later, and the net tree is what this row is reviewed as. **`4f22e9a7` (wrapped links):** `AppState::url_at_pane_cell` (`src/app/actions.rs:1999`) used to extract ONE viewport row (`Selection::range(pane_id, viewport_row, 0, width-1, metrics)`) and hand it straight to `url_at_column`, so a URL that soft-wrapped was two half-strings and neither half started with a scheme — clicking the tail found nothing. It now extracts the WHOLE visible block (`Selection::line_range` from `absolute_row_for_viewport(0)` to `absolute_row_for_viewport(height-1)`), maps the clicked SCREEN cell back to a LOGICAL cell through the new `logical_cell_for_visible_cell`, slices the logical line around that cell's `byte_index` on the surrounding newlines, and calls `url_at_column` with the cell's `logical_col`. `url_at_column` itself swaps `url_span_at_column(&cells, clicked_idx)?` for `url_spans(&cells).into_iter().find(\|span\| span.contains(clicked_idx))?` over the new `url_spans`, which is the same scan without the click filter; `url_span_at_column` stays because `word_bounds_at_column` still calls it. **The deferred `8d2af4e0` hunks 1-2 land here.** That commit (ported as `389bff5`, M2) converted the fork's width math from `unicode_width::UnicodeWidthChar` to libghostty's own table, but its two hunks for `visible_text_cells` and `logical_cell_for_visible_cell` were dropped because the fork had neither function yet; its ledger row records exactly that. Both arrive here written in ghostty form directly, never converted after the fact: `let width = u16::from(crate::ghostty::unicode_codepoint_width(ch as u32));` in `visible_text_cells` and `let width = u16::from(crate::ghostty::unicode_codepoint_width(cell.ch as u32));` in `logical_cell_for_visible_cell`. The `unicode_width` import is NOT re-added to `src/app/actions.rs`; `grep -rn 'UnicodeWidthChar' src/app/` is EMPTY after this commit, as it was before it. **`d6285aaa` (no render-time url scans):** everything `4f22e9a7` added to the render path is pruned before it is ever committed here, so the fork tree never carries `visible_url_spans` (`src/app/actions.rs`), `dirty_patch_contains_plain_url_candidate` or its `plain_url_candidate` retained-render fallback (`src/server/headless.rs`), or `plain_url_hyperlinks_for_pane` / `render_runtime_buffer` / `push_plain_url_line_links` / `pane_cell_is_visible` and the `final_buffer` parameter (`src/server/render_stream.rs`). **Render-purity result (§4.1):** the ONLY surviving production change in `src/server/render_stream.rs` is that `visible_hyperlinks` resolves each pane's runtime through `AppState::runtime_for_pane_in_workspace(terminal_runtimes, ws_idx, info.id)` instead of `active_tab().terminal_id(info.id)` plus a registry lookup — a lookup swap, not a scan, and it is what lets the function see a `#[cfg(test)]` test runtime at all. The scan did NOT move into a lazy cache read from the draw path: `grep -n 'RefCell\|Cell<\|AtomicBool' src/server/render_stream.rs` is EMPTY, and the only such hits under `src/ui*` are two pre-existing `AtomicBool` test constructors (`src/ui/mobile.rs:1369`, `src/ui/sidebar.rs:1540`) in files this commit does not touch. Plain-URL detection stays where it always was, on the input path (`url_at_pane_cell`, reached from `handle_modified_url_click`). **`58e812b2` (consume handled click gestures) — ADAPTED:** ctrl-clicking a URL consumed the `Down`, but the matching `Up` still reached a mouse-reporting pane as an unmatched SGR release, which alternate-screen apps read as a second click and open a duplicate browser tab. Upstream keys the suppression on a per-client input source (`pending_url_click_sources: HashSet<InputSourceId>`), but the fork has NO input-source multiplexing at all — `InputSourceId`, `LOCAL_INPUT_SOURCE`, `pressed_key_identity(source_id, key)`, `release_input_source`, `release_input_source_headless` and `clear_input_source` are all unported upstream additions, and `grep -rn 'InputSourceId' src` is empty at the base — so the fork's `repeat_key_identity` keys on the key alone and there is exactly one input path. The state therefore collapses to a single `App::pending_url_click: bool` (`src/app/mod.rs`), the gesture guard goes at the TOP of the existing `handle_mouse` (`src/app/input/mod.rs:225`, ahead of `handle_overlay_mouse`, which is the fork's first statement where upstream's is the `popup_pane` check) with the same three arms — a left `Down` clears the flag, a left `Drag` or `Up` while it is set is swallowed — and `handle_modified_url_click` keeps its one-argument signature and just sets the flag next to the existing `self.last_pane_click = None`. **DROPPED:** `handle_mouse_from_input_source` (nothing to thread a source through), the two `release_input_source*` hunks and the `handle_mouse_event_headless(source_id, mouse)` threading in `src/app/mod.rs` — none of those functions or parameters exist in the fork. Behaviour is identical on the fork's single input path; when the input-source feature is ported the `bool` becomes the upstream `HashSet` in one place. Upstream's `docs/next/CHANGELOG.md` hunk is dropped (`docs/next/` is untracked here). **Tests — RED first.** Nine upstream tests plus one fork-only oracle, added on the base before any production change; four were RED: `pane_cell_url_resolver_finds_soft_wrapped_url` and `pane_cell_url_resolver_handles_hard_newline_after_full_row` (`left: None, right: Some("https://example.com/...")`), `render_stream_exports_osc8_hyperlink_metadata` (panicked on `OSC 8 link cell` — the pre-change `active_tab().terminal_id()` lookup cannot see the test runtime) and the adapted `ctrl_click_url_does_not_forward_release_to_mouse_reporting_pane`, whose failure is the bug itself: the first byte the pane received was the leaked release `b"\x1b[<0;13;1m"` where the test expects the NEXT gesture's `b"\x1b[<0;1;1M"`. The other five are characterization, GREEN on the base by design because `d6285aaa` restores the base behaviour — the three `render_stream_does_not_synthesize_*` cases and `retained_pty_update_allows_dirty_row_that_creates_plain_url` assert that no plain-URL hyperlink metadata is ever synthesized, and `pane_cell_url_resolver_does_not_shift_after_zero_width_mark` already passed because `text_cells` handled the combining mark. The upstream click test is rewritten for one input source: instead of proving source 41's release is dropped while source 42's pass through, it sends ctrl-`Down` then `Up` on the URL and then a plain `Down`/`Up` elsewhere, asserting the pane sees ONLY the second gesture's two SGR reports and that the link handler fired exactly once; its URL is renamed to `https://github.com/dzevs/zynk/issues/1761` per ADR 0010 §7. **Fork-only:** `visible_text_cells_use_the_emulator_width_table` (`src/app/actions.rs`) pins the re-applied deferral — it walks `a` + two CJK ideographs + `b` + a combining acute + `c` and asserts the ghostty widths `[1,2,2,1,0,1]` produce `logical_col`/`screen_col` `[0,1,3,5,6,6]`, that a wide cell answers for BOTH of its columns while the zero-width mark answers only for the exact column it shares with the next character, and that at `pane_width = 4` two ideographs fill the row and the third wraps to `screen_row` 1. It was RED on the base as three `E0425` errors (`cannot find function visible_text_cells` / `logical_cell_for_visible_cell` in module `super`). **`NOTICE`:** `58e812b2` (2026-07-24) is POST-relicense — `git merge-base --is-ancestor cd5ea1be 58e812b2` succeeds, while the same check fails for `4f22e9a7` and `d6285aaa` — so its three modified files `src/app/input/mod.rs`, `src/app/input/terminal.rs` and `src/app/mod.rs` are appended to the §4(b) *Modified files* list in alphabetical position (52 entries, still sorted). **The M5 port sheet's §8 plan table mislabels M5-09 as `PRE` for all three commits; its §2.4/§2.5 commit tables are correct** and carry `58e812b2` under the `R` (post-relicense) heading — the `R` marking governs. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*` / `TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; `-E 'test(/url/) or test(/link/) or test(/input::terminal/) or test(/render_stream/) or test(/app::actions/)'` 183/183; `test(/zynk::header/)` 28/28; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test cross_area --test client_mode` 41/41; `method_wire_ids_are_stable` + `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` 2/2; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2768 -> 2778 passed, 1 skipped (+10 = 9 upstream + 1 fork-only).
