No per-file test count dropped: `src/app/actions.rs` 92 -> 93, `src/app/input/terminal.rs` 27 -> 35, `src/server/headless.rs` 91 -> 92, `src/app/mod.rs` 104 -> 104, `src/app/input/mod.rs` 5 -> 5; repo-wide `#[test]` 2826 -> 2827 and `#[tokio::test]` 192 -> 201. `just check` 3096/3096 passed, 2 skipped plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and 35 Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks); `python3 -m unittest scripts.test_release_audit_refs scripts.test_check_public_tree` 21/21 OK — the brief also named `scripts.test_license_docs`, which does NOT exist in this repo (`ModuleNotFoundError`); nothing under `scripts/` reads the `apache-modified-files` block at all, so that list is hand-maintained and was verified by hand to stay sorted. **Invariants:** `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs`, `src/ui.rs`, `src/app/creation.rs`, `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**` and `src/cli/**` untouched; `authoritative_receiver_identity` byte-identical (sha256 `14e5464168b4f34a...`); no `AppState`/`PaneState` field added (the one new field, `pending_url_click`, sits on the runtime `App`, not on `*State`, and is a plain `bool` — no channel, handle, lock or `Instant`); `render` is not touched; `install_db_workers` (def `src/server/headless.rs:3924`, calls `:1143`, `:3792`, `:4001`) is unchanged — the only `src/server/headless.rs` hunk is one added test. |
| *(this commit)* | `17e2b2b8` `1e1d0632` *(both pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **The configured prefix now works from copy mode, copy mode survives a focus move instead of being silently discarded, and navigator search stops eating both Escape meanings.** Two unrelated input fixes, one fork commit. **`17e2b2b8` (honor prefix in copy mode):** copy mode swallowed every key, so with the default prefix `ctrl+b` paged the viewport up and there was no way to reach a prefix action at all; and any focus move made while copy mode was open left `AppState::copy_mode` pointing at a pane that was no longer focused (or no longer alive). `App::handle_copy_mode_key` (`src/app/input/copy_mode.rs:14`) now tests `is_prefix_key` FIRST and switches to `Mode::Prefix` instead of dispatching the key, and six new `AppState` helpers in the same file carry the focus bookkeeping: `cancel_copy_mode` (`:173`, the public name for the existing `exit_copy_mode(.., false)`), `copy_mode_pane_is_focused` (`:507`), `sync_copy_mode_with_focus` (`:515` — re-enters `Mode::Copy` when the copy-mode pane is focused again, otherwise drops the selection and settles on `Terminal`/`Navigate`, and does nothing at all outside the four modes `Copy \| Terminal \| Navigate \| Prefix` so a modal is never torn down), `settle_terminal_mode_after_focus` (`:536`), `clear_copy_mode_selection` (`:541`) and `clear_copy_mode_for_removed_panes` (`:548`). In `src/app/actions.rs` the three navigation entry points `switch_workspace` (`:960`), `switch_workspace_tab` (`:983`) and `switch_tab` (`:1098`) stop blanket-clearing `selection` / `selection_autoscroll` at the top and instead end with the new `sync_selection_after_focus_navigation` (`:299`), which clears the selection exactly as before UNLESS copy mode is live, in which case `sync_copy_mode_with_focus` decides; `focus_pane_in_workspace` (`:307`) drops the copy-mode selection before the tab switch and re-syncs after the focus lands; `remove_plugin_pane_records` (`:1436`) grows from a `plugin_panes.remove` loop into the single removal chokepoint — it now also calls `clear_copy_mode_for_removed_panes` and drops a `previous_pane_focus` that points at one of the removed panes, which is what keeps `assert_invariants_for_test`'s "copy mode references missing pane" and "previous pane focus references missing pane" true after a close; and `handle_pane_died` (`:3014`) routes through it instead of calling `plugin_panes.remove` directly. Every `state.mode = Mode::Terminal` that follows a focus move becomes `settle_terminal_mode_after_focus()` — five sites in `src/app/api/panes.rs` (`:104`, `:165`, `:357`, `:942`, `:1094`), one in `src/app/agents.rs:41`, one in `src/app/api/plugins/mod.rs:408` and one in `src/app/input/mouse.rs:1447` — so `Mode` in `src/app/api/panes.rs` has no production use left and its import takes `#[cfg(test)]`. `AppState::set_pane_scroll_offset` (`src/app/input/mouse.rs:1733`) stops looking only in the ACTIVE workspace and scans all of them, because after a workspace switch the copy-mode pane whose scroll must be restored lives in a background workspace. On the prefix side, `App::handle_prefix_key` (`src/app/input/navigate.rs:45`) gains the policy: pressing the prefix twice cancels copy mode before the pass-through, `NavigateAction::CopyMode` and `EditScrollback` and every custom command cancel it first (new `cancel_copy_mode_if_active`, `:39`), the 17 focus-only actions listed by `copy_mode_survives_prefix_action` (`:1279`) keep it and re-sync afterwards, and everything else cancels; `leave_command_mode` (`:1697`) returns to `Mode::Copy` rather than `Mode::Terminal` when the copy-mode pane still holds focus, which is what makes prefix-then-Escape a no-op. `src/ui/panes.rs` is a test-hygiene hunk only — `pane_border_renderer_places_adjacent_cjk_by_display_width` takes the tab's real `root_pane` instead of assuming `PaneId::from_raw(1)`. **`1e1d0632` (preserve navigator search commands):** in `handle_navigator_key` (`src/app/input/modal.rs:160`) Escape inside the search field cleared the query, the state filter and the focus at once (or closed the picker when the query was empty), and `/` rebuilt search mode by clearing the query — so there was no way back to command mode with the filtered rows intact and no way to resume editing a filter. Escape in the search field now only blurs it, Escape in command mode always closes the picker, and `/` refocuses the existing query untouched (it still clears the state filter). `render_footer` (`src/ui/navigator.rs:475`) picks its shortcut line from `app.navigator.search_focused` so command keys are not advertised while the text input owns them; it stays a pure read of `&AppState` with no mutation. **Adaptations.** (1) Upstream's `docs/next/website/src/content/docs/keyboard.mdx` hunk is dropped — `docs/next/` is gitignored here (`.gitignore:25`) — and nothing tracked went stale: the fork's only prose about copy mode (`README.md:188`) names `prefix+[`, `v`, `y` and `q` and never claimed `ctrl+b` pages. (2) **The fork never carried upstream `fbd20ad6`'s API-layer `plugin_panes.remove` loops**, so upstream's "loop -> `remove_plugin_pane_records`" rewrite of `src/app/api/tabs.rs` and `src/app/api/workspaces.rs` has no anchor here: `git log -S'plugin_panes' -- src/app/api/tabs.rs src/app/api/workspaces.rs` is EMPTY in this repo, and `src/app/api/panes.rs::handle_pane_close` had no such call either. Before this commit that was only a leaked `plugin_panes` entry; now that the same call clears copy mode and `previous_pane_focus`, it is a behaviour hole — closing a tab or a pane over the socket while that pane held copy mode left `copy_mode` dangling on a removed pane. The call is therefore ADDED at the upstream post-image positions, `src/app/api/tabs.rs:243` and `src/app/api/panes.rs:1465`, the first collecting its ids through the fork-native `AppState::pane_ids_for_tab` (`src/app/actions.rs:1396`) rather than upstream's inline `tab.layout.pane_ids()`. `handle_workspace_close` is deliberately left untouched: it calls `close_selected_workspace` (`src/app/actions.rs:1454`), which already runs `remove_plugin_pane_records` over every pane it closes, so upstream's second call there is pure redundancy and adding it would be dead parity. The fork's compensating `self.state.plugin_panes.remove(&pane_id)` in `src/app/api/plugins/mod.rs:451` stays — `remove_plugin_pane_records` is idempotent. **Tests — RED first.** The eleven upstream tests were added on the M5-09 base before any production change and ten of them failed there: `copy_mode_prefix_takes_priority_over_ctrl_b_page_up`, `copy_mode_prefix_escape_returns_to_copy_mode`, `copy_mode_prefix_focus_keeps_copy_mode_on_source_pane`, `copy_mode_focus_away_preserves_scrollback_position`, `copy_mode_clears_when_source_tab_closes_after_focus_away` and `copy_mode_same_tab_switch_preserves_selection` (`src/app/input/copy_mode.rs`), `api_pane_move_focuses_copy_mode_pane_back_into_copy_mode` and `api_pane_zoom_focuses_copy_mode_pane_back_into_copy_mode` (`src/app/api/panes.rs`), `navigator_empty_search_escape_returns_to_commands` and `navigator_search_escape_blurs_then_next_escape_closes` (`src/app/input/modal.rs`) — the copy-mode and pane ones all failing `left: Copy, right: Terminal` or its mirror because the base treated the prefix as a page key and reset the mode unconditionally, the two navigator ones because Escape closed the picker instead of blurring. The eleventh, `copy_mode_cancel_restores_scroll_after_workspace_switch`, cannot be expressed on the base at all (`AppState::cancel_copy_mode` does not exist) and was added with the implementation. Upstream's edit to the pre-existing `copy_mode_ctrl_b_uses_page_up` — moving the prefix to `ctrl+a` so the test still exercises paging — was applied in the RED phase and stayed green there, which is the point: the fix changes what `ctrl+b` means only because it is the DEFAULT prefix. Two fork-only tests pin adaptation (2): `api_tab_close_clears_copy_mode_for_removed_panes` (`src/app/api/tabs.rs`) and `api_pane_close_clears_copy_mode_for_removed_pane` (`src/app/api/panes.rs`) each put copy mode on the pane they then close over the API and assert `copy_mode.is_none()` plus a clean `assert_invariants_for_test()`; both were RED on the base on `assertion failed: app.state.copy_mode.is_none()`. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*` / `TMPDIR`):** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; `-E 'test(/prefix/) \| test(/copy_mode/) \| test(/navigator/) \| test(/input::modal/) \| test(/input::mouse/) \| test(/input::navigate/) \| test(/api::panes/) \| test(/app::api/)'` 339/339; `test(/zynk::header/)` 28/28; `method_wire_ids_are_stable` + `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` 2/2 with the same 80 wire ids (no `Method` variant added or renamed); `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test cross_area --test client_mode` 41/41; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2778 -> 2791 passed, 1 skipped (+13 = 11 upstream + 2 fork-only); `just check` 3096 -> 3109 passed, 2 skipped plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and 35 Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). No per-file `#[test]` / `#[tokio::test]` count dropped and no test moved between files: `src/app/input/copy_mode.rs` 17 -> 24, `src/app/api/panes.rs` 38 -> 41, `src/app/input/modal.rs` 27 -> 29, `src/app/api/tabs.rs` 1 -> 2, with `src/app/actions.rs` 93, `src/app/input/mouse.rs` 54, `src/app/input/navigate.rs` 56, `src/ui/panes.rs` 17, `src/app/api/plugins/mod.rs` 43 and `src/app/api/workspaces.rs` 3 unchanged; repo-wide `#[test]` 2827 -> 2833 and `#[tokio::test]` 201 -> 208. **Invariants:** `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs`, `src/ui.rs`, `src/app/creation.rs`, `src/server/headless.rs`, `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/app/api/workspaces.rs`, `src/zynk/**` and `src/cli/**` are untouched by this commit (`git diff --quiet` per path), so no `AppState`/`PaneState` field is added, `render` is not touched, `install_db_workers` is unchanged and `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (sha256 `9430e4c6ec415e59...`); the one `src/ui/**` production hunk, `render_footer`, only reads `app.navigator.search_focused`. `NOTICE` unchanged — `git merge-base --is-ancestor cd5ea1be <sha>` fails for both commits, so both predate the relicense. |
| *(this commit)* | `b425260f` *(pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Session autosave leaves the main loop: the debounced save now serializes and writes on a named background thread, so a large snapshot no longer stalls input or render, and the spawn handle lives on the runtime `App` rather than on the pure `AppState`.** **Mechanism.** `src/app/session.rs` splits the old single `save_session_now` into a capture half and a write half. A new private `SessionSaveJob` enum (`:5`) carries either `Clear` or `Save { snapshot, history }`; `App::capture_session_save_job` (`:39`) holds the `&self` reads that must happen on the main thread — `crate::persist::capture` over `state.workspaces` / `state.terminals` / `terminal_runtimes` plus the `persist_pane_history`-gated `capture_history` — and returns an owned, `Send` job. The free function `run_session_save_job` (`:101`) is the only writer and just fans out to `crate::persist::clear()` or `crate::persist::save(&snapshot, history.as_ref())`. `App::start_background_session_save` (`:60`) is the new deadline handler: it short-circuits under `no_session` (clearing the deadline, as before), calls `reap_finished_session_save` (`:27` — joins the handle only when `JoinHandle::is_finished`, so it never blocks), and if a writer is still in flight it COALESCES by pushing `session_save_deadline` out `250ms` and returning without capturing anything. Otherwise it captures the job, clears the deadline, and spawns via `std::thread::Builder::new().name("zynk-session-save".into())`; a spawn failure is not swallowed — it logs `tracing::warn!` and falls back to an inline `run_session_save_job(self.capture_session_save_job())`, so a save is never silently dropped. `save_session_now` (`:86`) keeps its name and its `pub(crate)` visibility but becomes the join-then-write path: it takes and joins any outstanding handle FIRST, then applies the `no_session` short-circuit, then writes inline. **Call sites.** The two due-deadline sites swap to the background entry — the TUI loop `App::handle_scheduled_tasks` (`src/app/runtime.rs:286`) and the headless loop (`src/server/headless.rs:3523`). The two EXIT-path sites deliberately stay on `save_session_now`, `src/app/mod.rs:1056` and `src/server/headless.rs:540`, which is what makes shutdown join the writer before the process leaves the loop. `schedule_session_save` and the fork-native `sync_session_save_schedule` are carried through verbatim. **State purity (the point of this commit).** Moving work off the loop adds a spawn handle, and the handle goes on the RUNTIME: `App::session_save_thread: Option<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>>` (`src/app/mod.rs:136`) sits directly beside the existing `session_save_deadline`, initialised `None` at `:726`. Upstream's diffstat carries no `src/app/state.rs` hunk and neither does this one — `git diff --numstat BASE..HEAD -- src/app/state.rs src/pane/state.rs` is EMPTY, so `AppState` and `PaneState` remain channel-free, handle-free pure data and `AppState::test_new()` still constructs with no runtime plumbing. **Adaptations.** (1) The thread name `herdr-session-save` becomes `zynk-session-save` (ADR 0010 §7 CI gate). (2) The test helper `unique_temp_path` formats `zynk-{name}-{pid}-{stamp}` rather than `herdr-…`; upstream inserts it above the `#[cfg(windows)]` / `#[cfg(not(windows))]` `exiting_test_command` pair, which does not exist here — ADR 0013 collapsed that to one Linux arm — so it is inserted above the single-arm fork version instead. (3) Upstream's `std::env::remove_var(crate::session::SESSION_ENV_VAR)` is insufficient in the fork: `crate::session::data_dir()` resolves through `active_name()`, which the fork additionally gates on the `EXPLICIT_SESSION_REQUESTED` static, so the test pairs the `remove_var` with `crate::session::clear_explicit_session_for_test()` — the established fork pattern (`src/session.rs:1017`) — and the save therefore lands at `$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/zynk-dev/session.json` rather than under a stale `sessions/<name>/` subdirectory. No other adaptation: `src/app/session.rs` was byte-identical to upstream's pre-image, so the production half applies unchanged apart from the thread name. **Tests — RED first.** The three upstream tests plus one fork-only oracle were added on the M5-10 base BEFORE any production change and all four were RED, as 9 compile errors: 1 × `E0599` (`no method named start_background_session_save found for struct app::App`) and 8 × `E0609` (`no field session_save_thread on type app::App`). `due_session_save_starts_background_writer` drives a due deadline through `handle_scheduled_tasks` and asserts the handle exists, the deadline is cleared, and that the follow-up `save_session_now` leaves a real `session.json` on disk. `background_session_save_reschedules_when_writer_is_busy` parks a fake writer on a channel and asserts the coalescing branch keeps the handle AND re-arms the deadline instead of spawning a second writer. `final_session_save_joins_background_writer_before_returning` proves the exit path blocks: the fake writer only completes 30ms later, yet `done_rx.try_recv()` succeeds immediately after `save_session_now` returns and the handle is `None`. **Fork-only:** `background_session_save_handle_lives_on_app_not_state` (`src/app/mod.rs`) is the state-purity oracle — it constructs `AppState::test_new()` unchanged, then plants and drains a handle through `App` alone, pinning the field to the runtime side so a future port cannot quietly move it onto `AppState`. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*`).** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; `-E 'test(/session/) | test(/app::runtime/) | test(/server::headless/) | test(/persist/)'` 347/347; `test(/zynk::receipt/)` 29/29; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test detach_reattach --test live_handoff` 54/54 (the known `live_server_holds_one_pty_master_fd_per_pane` flake did NOT fire); `method_wire_ids_are_stable` + `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` 2/2; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2791 -> 2795 passed, 1 skipped (+4 = 3 upstream + 1 fork-only); `just check` 3109 -> 3113 passed, 2 skipped plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and the Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). No per-file `#[test]` / `#[tokio::test]` count dropped and no test moved between files: `src/app/mod.rs` 104 -> 108, with `src/app/session.rs` 0, `src/app/runtime.rs` 12, `src/server/headless.rs` 92 and `src/app/state.rs` 9 unchanged; repo-wide `#[test]` 2833 -> 2837 and `#[tokio::test]` 208 -> 208. **Invariants.** `src/app/state.rs`, `src/pane/state.rs`, `src/ui.rs`, `src/app/creation.rs`, `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**` and `src/cli/**` are untouched by this commit (`git diff --quiet` per path), so no `AppState`/`PaneState` field is added, `render` is not touched and no `compute_*` boundary moves; `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (sha256 `b3234f2687b86f37…`); `install_db_workers` is unchanged — its definition is byte-identical and it still sits at `src/server/headless.rs:3924` with the same three calls at `:1143`, `:3792` and `:4001`, because the ONLY `src/server/headless.rs` hunk in this commit is the one-line swap at `:3523`. `NOTICE` unchanged — `git merge-base --is-ancestor cd5ea1be b425260f` fails, so the commit predates the relicense. |
| *(this commit)* | `c8850a2e` `b0d46fb9` *(both pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Copy mode learns literal search (`/` `?` `n` `N`) and real tmux word motions, and both stop reading one rendered viewport row and start reading the emulator's own text; `$` then stops at the last character on the line instead of the right edge.** Two commits, one fork commit, applied in upstream order — `b0d46fb9` (2026-07-14) only makes sense on top of `c8850a2e`'s rewrite of `copy_mode_line_edge`. **Mechanism (`c8850a2e`).** The old motions worked on `copy_mode_visible_row_text` — ONE viewport row, re-extracted per keystroke — so `w`/`b`/`e` could never leave the row, a soft-wrapped word was two words, and there was no search at all. Four layers replace that. (1) **The emulator exposes rows.** `src/ghostty/mod.rs` gains `ScreenTextCell { wide, graphemes }` and `ScreenTextRow { cells, soft_wrapped, wrap_continuation }` plus `Terminal::screen_text_rows()` / `screen_text_rows_range(start, end_exclusive)`, which walk `grid_ref` over SCREEN coordinates and read each row's wrap flags through the new `grid_ref_wrap_state` helper over `ghostty_row_get` with `GHOSTTY_ROW_DATA_WRAP` / `GHOSTTY_ROW_DATA_WRAP_CONTINUATION` (both already in the vendored `screen.h` and `bindings.rs`; no vendor change). (2) **`src/pane/terminal.rs` (+764) turns rows into text.** A private `RetainedTextBuffer` is built in one of two shapes — `new_search` (logical LINES: soft-wrapped rows concatenate, hard breaks split, trailing blanks trimmed, every cell keeping a byte-range → `TerminalTextPoint` span) or `new_words` (ATOMS: one entry per non-`SpacerTail` cell classed `Whitespace` / `Separator` / `Word` by the tmux separator set `!\"#$%&'()*+,-./:;<=>?@[\\]^`{\|}~`, with a `None`-point sentinel at each hard line end). `search` runs an escaped `regex` over the logical text, case-insensitive unless the query holds an uppercase letter, and returns `TerminalTextMatch { start, end, source_fingerprint, scan_cols, scan_screen }`; `word_motion` walks atoms for `NextStart` / `PreviousStart` / `NextEnd`. `PaneTerminal` exposes `search_text_matches`, `word_motion_target` and the freshness pair `text_match_is_current` / `text_matches_are_current`, which re-read ONLY the rows a match spans and compare a `DefaultHasher` fingerprint of the text, so a match whose cells were overwritten, whose column count changed, or whose `ActiveScreen` flipped is dropped instead of highlighted. `word_motion_target` starts on a 64-row window and DOUBLES it while the window's first row is a `wrap_continuation` (for `PreviousStart`) or its last row is `soft_wrapped` and the target is the final atom (for `NextEnd`), so a motion never stops at an arbitrary window edge. (3) **Plumbing:** `src/pane.rs` re-exports `TerminalTextMatch`, `TerminalTextPoint`, `TerminalWordMotion` and `PaneRuntime` forwards the four methods; `src/terminal/runtime.rs` forwards the same four. (4) **State + input.** `CopyModeState` loses `Copy` and gains `search: CopyModeSearchState { prompt, query, direction, matches, current, geometry }` (`src/app/state.rs`), which is why nearly every `let Some(mut copy_mode) = self.copy_mode` in `src/app/input/copy_mode.rs` becomes a borrow plus a write-back through `self.copy_mode.as_mut()`. `/` and `?` open the prompt, Enter submits through `submit_copy_mode_search`, `n`/`N` repeat via `repeat_copy_mode_search` (`N` reverses through the new `CopyModeSearchDirection::reversed`), `search_match_index` picks the next match after the cursor and wraps to the ends, and `move_copy_cursor_to_absolute` scrolls the pane so the target row is visible (reserving the overlay row for a search jump, not for a word motion). Esc is now two-stage: it CLEARS an active selection or search first and only exits copy mode when both are empty. `word_motion_target`, `word_spans` and `WordSpan` are DELETED — `copy_mode_word_motion` now asks the runtime with an absolute row. `src/app/input/mod.rs` routes bracketed paste into the prompt (`Mode::Copy` arms in `insert_paste_text` + `modal_paste_target_active`), `src/ui/menus.rs` renders the prompt line and a ` current/total` match counter next to `n/N repeat` in the COPY overlay, and `src/ui/panes.rs` paints the non-current matches UNDER the selection highlight and the current match OVER it, after `validated_copy_mode_search_matches` has narrowed the list to the visible rows and run it through `text_matches_are_current`. **Render purity (§4.1):** the mutation the feature needs is `AppState::sync_copy_mode_search_geometry`, and it is called from `compute_view_internal` and `compute_mobile_view` (`src/ui.rs:326`, `:397`) — both `compute_*` functions — never from `render`, which this commit does not touch; the two `src/ui/panes.rs` renderers only read `app.copy_mode` and the runtime. **State purity:** `CopyModeSearchState` is plain owned data — two `Option`s, a `String`, a `Vec<TerminalTextMatch>`, an `Option<usize>` and an `Option<(u16, u16)>`. `git diff -U0 -- src/app/state.rs src/pane/state.rs` added lines carry NO `Sender`, `Receiver`, `JoinHandle`, `Arc<`, `Mutex`, `RwLock`, `Instant`, `AtomicBool` or `tokio::` (grep empty), and `src/pane/state.rs` is untouched, so `AppState::test_new()` still constructs with no runtime plumbing. **`b0d46fb9` (line-end stops at text):** `copy_mode_line_edge(.., end = true)` set `cursor_col` to `info.inner_rect.width - 1`, so `$` on a five-character line parked the cursor in trailing blank cells; it now reads the row text and takes `last_character_col(&text).unwrap_or(0)` clamped to the pane width. **The deferred `8d2af4e0` hunks 3-4 land here.** That commit (ported as `389bff5`, M2) moved the fork's width math onto libghostty's table, but its two `src/app/input/copy_mode.rs` hunks were dropped because the fork had neither `last_character_col` nor copy-mode search yet; its ledger row records exactly that. **Hunk 3** arrives written in ghostty form directly, never converted after the fact — the body line is `let width = u16::from(crate::ghostty::unicode_codepoint_width(ch as u32));`, NOT upstream's `UnicodeWidthChar::width(ch).unwrap_or(1) as u16`, and `use unicode_width::UnicodeWidthChar;` is NOT re-added. The behaviour differs and the difference is intended: the emulator table returns 0 for C0/C1 controls, combining marks, default-ignorables and surrogates, where `unwrap_or(1)` charges a column for every codepoint `unicode-width` reports as `None` — the same swap already shipped for `char_cell_width` (`src/app/input/copy_mode.rs:952`) in M2. `grep -rn 'UnicodeWidthChar' src/app/` is EMPTY after this commit, as it was before it. **Hunk 4** is the viewport assertion in the search test: after `submit_copy_search(&mut app, '?', "000000")` the test now also asserts `copy_mode_viewport_top_row(&app, pane_id) + usize::from(copy_mode.cursor_row)` equals `0`, in the `8d2af4e0` three-line form, so a backward search that scrolls into history is pinned to the absolute row it landed on and not merely to a non-zero scroll offset. **Adaptations.** (1) `src/pane/terminal.rs`'s header hunk was re-applied by hand: upstream's context is `use std::time::{Duration, Instant};` and the fork's line 3 is `use std::time::Duration;`, so `use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;` and `use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};` were inserted above `use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};` instead. (2) The `word_motion_target` + `word_spans` deletion hunk and `b0d46fb9`'s `last_character_col` insertion hunk both carry upstream's `char_cell_width` body as trailing context, which the fork already ships in ghostty form — both were re-applied by hand, the two functions removed and `char_cell_width` left untouched. (3) Two FORK-ONLY test sites build `CopyModeState` by literal and needed the new field: `api_pane_close_clears_copy_mode_for_removed_pane` (`src/app/api/panes.rs`) and `api_tab_close_clears_copy_mode_for_removed_panes` (`src/app/api/tabs.rs`) each gain `search: Default::default(),`, matching the two upstream sites the commit does touch; upstream has neither test, so it carries no hunk for them. (4) `src/pane.rs` needed no re-anchoring at all: the fork's M4 launch-env code and `publish_state_changed_event` sit elsewhere in `impl PaneRuntime`, and both hunks applied on their upstream context (`scroll_metrics` above, `input_state` below) at an offset of -122 lines. **DROPPED:** upstream's five doc hunks — `docs/next/CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/next/README.md` and the three `keyboard.mdx` translations — because `docs/next/` is untracked here and the fork's tracked `README.md:188` names only `v`/`y`/`q`, never the motions, exactly as recorded for M5-06. **Tests — RED first.** All 26 tests were added on the M5-11 base BEFORE any production change and the tree did not compile: 78 errors, 25 in `src/app/input/copy_mode.rs`, 50 in `src/pane/terminal.rs`, 2 in `src/app/api/panes.rs` and 1 in `src/ghostty/mod.rs` (`E0422` 20, `E0609` 16, `E0433` 15, `E0599` 13, `E0425` 12, `E0560` 2). 25 are upstream: 12 copy-mode cases (`copy_mode_search_wraps_and_repeats_in_both_directions` — which carries hunk 4 — plus backward search, shifted-slash, escape-clears-search, escape-clears-selection, prompt escape/failure, prompt paste, selection extension, resize-clears-matches, hidden-source, live-follow and `copy_mode_word_motions_cross_rows_and_respect_separators`), `copy_mode_line_end_stops_at_last_character` from `b0d46fb9`, `screen_text_rows_preserve_wrap_and_grapheme_cells` in `src/ghostty/mod.rs`, and 11 `src/pane/terminal.rs` cases over `RetainedTextBuffer` and the live-freshness path. **Fork-only:** `last_character_col_uses_the_emulator_width_table` (`src/app/input/copy_mode.rs`) is the oracle for hunk 3 — it pins `unicode_codepoint_width` to 0 for `U+0301` and `U+0007` and to 2 for `界`, then asserts `last_character_col` reports `Some(1)` for `"ae\u{301}"` (the mark takes no column), `None` for a lone combining mark, `Some(1)` for `"a界"` and `Some(2)` for `"界a"` (the wide character spans two), and `Some(1)` for `"a\u{7}b"` — the discriminating case, since the `unicode-width` form would charge the BEL a column and answer `Some(2)`. It was RED on the base as six `E0425` (`cannot find function last_character_col in this scope`). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*`).** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; `-E 'test(/copy_mode/) \| test(/pane::terminal/) \| test(/ghostty/) \| test(/api::panes/) \| test(/ui/)'` 435/435; `test(/detect::/)` 135/135 (`src/pane.rs` is shared with detection); `test(/zynk::header/)` 28/28; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 52/52; `--test server_headless --test cross_area` 26/26; `method_wire_ids_are_stable` + `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` 2/2; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2795 -> 2821 passed, 1 skipped (+26 = 25 upstream + 1 fork-only); `just check` 3113 -> 3139 passed, 2 skipped plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and 35 Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). No per-file `#[test]` / `#[tokio::test]` count dropped and no test moved between files: `src/app/input/copy_mode.rs` 0/24 -> 1/37, `src/ghostty/mod.rs` 27 -> 28, `src/pane/terminal.rs` 95 -> 106, with `src/app/api/panes.rs` 35/6, `src/app/api/tabs.rs` 2/0, `src/app/input/mod.rs` 3/2, `src/app/state.rs` 9/0, `src/pane.rs` 56/7, `src/ui.rs` 27/4 and `src/ui/panes.rs` 12/5 unchanged; repo-wide `#[test]` 2837 -> 2850 and `#[tokio::test]` 208 -> 221. **Invariants.** `src/pane/state.rs`, `src/app/creation.rs`, `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**`, `src/cli/**` and `src/server/headless.rs` are untouched by this commit (`git diff --quiet` per path), so `authoritative_receiver_identity` is byte-identical (sha256 `9db3813ce7bf0c81…`, same value on `528e170`) and `install_db_workers` and its three call sites are unchanged; `render` is not touched and no `compute_*` boundary moves; `NOTICE` unchanged — `git merge-base --is-ancestor cd5ea1be <sha>` fails for both `c8850a2e` and `b0d46fb9`, so both predate the relicense. |
| *(this commit)* | *(none — fork-only test harness fix)* | **Test-only (sandbox root): the sixteen integration harnesses stop hardcoding `/tmp` and build their sandbox under one overridable root, so a machine whose `/tmp` is a quota-limited tmpfs can run the suite off RAM.** **Why.** Every harness built its base directory from a literal `/tmp/...` and ignored `TMPDIR`, which pinned the whole integration suite to tmpfs with no way out. On this workstation `/tmp` is a 32 GiB tmpfs under a per-user quota of 25570 MB, and `df` is misleading because the quota binds first; a parallel run under load failed roughly 39 tests with `Os { code: 122, kind: QuotaExceeded }` at harness setup, before any zynk process started, and every run cost RAM. When this commit was written `/tmp` held 17 GiB across 39422 leftover sandbox directories, 8316 of them `zynk-receipt-test-*`. `/tmp` was chosen originally for the Unix-socket path budget — `sun_path` is 108 bytes (SUN_LEN) and a long root makes `api::server` tests fail to BIND rather than fail an assertion — so the fix makes the root overridable and documents the length constraint instead of simply relocating it. **Mechanism.** `tests/support/mod.rs` gains `pub fn test_root() -> PathBuf` (`:94`): it returns `$ZYNK_TEST_ROOT` when that variable is set and non-blank, otherwise `/tmp` exactly as before, and `create_dir_all`s the result. The resolution itself lives in the private pure core `test_root_from(Option<&str>)` (`:103`), split out so the guard test can exercise both branches WITHOUT mutating the process environment — under `cargo test` the harnesses run their tests as threads in one process, where an env write would race live sandbox setup (nextest's process-per-test would have hidden that). The doc comment carries the two constraints a caller can get wrong: keep the root SHORT (the sandbox holds `<root>/<harness>-<pid>-<nanos>/runtime/zynk-client.sock`), and make it a REAL directory, never a symlink — several tests compare a pane's reported cwd, which the kernel reports resolved, against the sandbox path built from this root, so a symlinked root makes the two spellings differ (observed: `api_ping::agent_start_creates_named_terminal_over_socket` and `pane_info_reports_foreground_cwd_without_changing_pane_cwd` both fail on the equality). **Routing.** Sixteen call sites now read `support::test_root().join(...)`, each preserving its existing name/pid/nanos suffix scheme byte-for-byte apart from the root: `tests/api_ping.rs:21` (`hapi-`), `tests/auto_detect.rs:25`, `tests/cli_wrapper.rs:21` (`hcli-`), `tests/client_mode.rs:27`, `tests/cross_area.rs:26`, `tests/detach_reattach.rs:27`, `tests/live_handoff.rs:47` (`hlh-`), `tests/multi_client.rs:27`, `tests/server_headless.rs:25`, `tests/zynk_db_cutover.rs:47`, `tests/zynk_embed.rs:37`, `tests/zynk_header.rs:60`, `tests/zynk_message.rs:43`, `tests/zynk_native_cli.rs:54`, `tests/zynk_query.rs:51` and `tests/zynk_receipt.rs:44`. Four of those (`api_ping`, `auto_detect`, `cli_wrapper`, `cross_area`) were not on the original defect list and were found by grepping `tests/` rather than trusting it. **Adaptations.** `tests/zynk_embed.rs` was the one harness without `mod support;`; it gains the declaration (`:22`) rather than a duplicated helper, so exactly ONE definition of the root exists — the side effect is that the five pre-existing `support::tests::*` cases now also compile into that binary. **Deliberately untouched.** The `"cwd": "/tmp"` request parameters in `tests/live_handoff.rs` and `tests/cli_wrapper.rs` are pane working directories, not sandbox roots; so are the session fixtures and `/tmp/zynk-clihelp-test-nonexistent.sock`, a path whose whole purpose is to NOT exist. The `src/` unit tests that reach `/tmp` do so through `std::env::temp_dir()`, which already honours `TMPDIR`, so they are a separate control surface and out of this commit's scope — they remain the larger `/tmp` consumer during a full `just check` (`hh-*` from `src/server/headless.rs:4078`, plus `zynk-receipt-test-*`, `zynk-persistence-test-*`, `zynk-embed-worker-test-*`, `zynk-vector-test-*` and `zynk-fts-trace-test-*` from `src/zynk/**`). **Docs.** `CLAUDE.md` §Testing gains one bullet naming `ZYNK_TEST_ROOT`, the tmpfs motivation, and both constraints (absolute real directory, kept short for the 108-byte `sun_path` budget). **Tests — RED first.** The guard test `support::tests::test_root_honours_the_override_and_stays_inside_the_socket_budget` (`tests/support/mod.rs:808`) was written on the M5-12 base BEFORE the helper existed and was RED as 5 compile errors, all `error[E0425]` (4 × `cannot find function test_root_from in this scope`, 1 × `cannot find function test_root in this scope`), `cargo nextest run --locked --test server_headless -E 'test(test_root_honours)'` exit 101. It asserts an override is taken verbatim, that both an absent and a blank value fall back to `/tmp`, that `test_root()` resolves through the same pure core as `test_root_from` (so the two branches are the branches the harnesses take, without reading an ambient value), and that the worst-case socket path built from either root stays under a 100-byte budget — 7 digits of pid (Linux `pid_max`, 2^22) and 19 digits of `as_nanos()` (valid to year 2262) under the longest harness prefix `zynk-multi-client-test`, failing with a message that names the budget, SUN_LEN and a shorter example. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*`; `ZYNK_TEST_ROOT` on disk).** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; the sixteen harnesses 318 -> 339 tests, 339 passed and 1 skipped in one fully parallel run with NO `--test-threads 1` and no retry — the known environment-limited `live_server_holds_one_pty_master_fd_per_pane` did not fire; the test-set diff is +21 and -0 (16 × the new guard test, one per harness, plus the 5 pre-existing `support::tests::*` cases newly compiled into `zynk_embed`); the full `--bin zynk` suite 2821 passed and 1 skipped, unchanged, since this commit adds no unit test; `just check` 3139 -> 3160 passed, 2 skipped, plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and 35 Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). **Routing proof.** Two harnesses were run twice under `inotifywait` watching both locations. With `ZYNK_TEST_ROOT` set: 33/33 passed, 21 sandbox directories created under the override and 0 under `/tmp`, with the `/tmp` counts for both prefixes unchanged. With the variable unset: 33/33 passed, 21 sandbox directories under `/tmp` and 0 under the override — the default is byte-for-byte the old behaviour. **Invariants.** No `src/` file is touched, so no production behaviour changes and no `AppState`/`PaneState`, detection, receipt or wire-id surface moves; `git diff --stat` covers `tests/**`, `CLAUDE.md` and this ledger only. `NOTICE` unchanged — this commit has no upstream counterpart. |
| *(this commit)* | *(none — fork-only test containment fix)* | **Test-only (fixture git containment): a fixture's `git init` and identity write can no longer reach a repository the fixture does not own, so a test run inside a real checkout stops authoring later commits as `Zynk Test <zynk@example.invalid>`.** No production line changes. **The defect.** Every fixture helper seeded a temp repo with `git -C <path> config user.email zynk@example.invalid` and `user.name "Zynk Test"`. `git config` WALKS UP to the nearest parent repository when `<path>` is not itself a repository, so a fixture whose `git init` did not take, or whose root happens to sit inside a checkout, writes those two values into the REAL repository's `.git/config`. Observed twice in this program's shared checkout, each time right after a `just check` run made inside it; every later commit there without an explicit identity is then authored `Zynk Test <zynk@example.invalid>`. **The trigger (independently reproduced by the Gate-3 arbiter, `msg_8b73579479220603`, and again here before the fix).** With `GIT_DIR=<outer>/.git` INHERITED in the environment, `git -C <child> init --quiet` exits 0 while leaving `<child>/.git` ABSENT — git initialises the directory `GIT_DIR` names, not the child — after which `git -C <child> config user.email …` also exits 0 and lands in the OUTER repository. Every status code is success, so nothing in the fixture notices; a caller whose environment happens to be sanitised never sees it, which is why the fix cannot depend on a clean environment. **Three layers, all kept.** (1) **Scrub** — `fixture_git_command()` builds every fixture git `Command` with `env_remove` of `GIT_DIR`, `GIT_WORK_TREE`, `GIT_INDEX_FILE`, `GIT_COMMON_DIR`, `GIT_OBJECT_DIRECTORY`, `GIT_ALTERNATE_OBJECT_DIRECTORIES`, `GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES` and `GIT_DISCOVERY_ACROSS_FILESYSTEM`, plus `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null` and `GIT_CONFIG_SYSTEM=/dev/null` so the host's own git settings cannot steer a fixture either. This prevents the shape from arising. (2) **Containment assert** — `fixture_git_config_path(repo)` asserts `repo.join(".git").exists()` BEFORE any config write, with a message naming the path, so a skipped or hijacked `git init` aborts the test instead of escaping. This catches the shape if it arises anyway. (3) **Unescapable write** — the identity is written with `git config --file <config> …`, which cannot walk up at all; the config path is resolved with `rev-parse --path-format=absolute --git-common-dir`, so a linked worktree (whose `.git` is a FILE) resolves to the main repository's own config rather than to `.git/worktrees/<name>`, which git would ignore. **Hardened call sites.** Identity seeding now goes through `seed_fixture_identity` at all seven sites — `src/worktree.rs:516`, `src/app/worktrees.rs:995`, `src/app/api/worktrees.rs:853`, `src/workspace/git/status.rs:407` and `:433`, `src/workspace/git/discovery.rs:464`, `tests/cli_wrapper.rs:111` — and `grep -rn 'zynk@example.invalid' --include='*.rs'` now finds no raw `run_git(&repo, &["config", …])` anywhere. The scrub reaches further than the identity sites, because the same inherited `GIT_DIR` also redirects a bare `git init` or an empty commit: `fixture_git_command()` now backs `run_git` in `src/worktree.rs`, `src/app/worktrees.rs`, `src/app/api/worktrees.rs`, `src/workspace/git/test_support.rs` (shared by `status.rs` and `discovery.rs`) and `tests/cli_wrapper.rs`; the three `git init --ref-format=reftable` spawns in `src/workspace/git/status.rs:399` and `src/workspace/git/discovery.rs:343` / `:455`; the `branch --show-current` probes in `src/worktree.rs`, `src/app/worktrees.rs` and `src/app/api/worktrees.rs`; and four fixtures that create a repo without writing an identity — `init_repo` in `src/app/api.rs` and `src/server/notifications.rs`, the inline `git init` in `src/app/runtime.rs`, and the throwaway repo in `src/zynk/message.rs::git_meta_in_a_temp_repo_returns_branch_and_sha`, whose `commit --allow-empty` would otherwise have landed in the outer repository. Those four also gain a `.git`-exists assert straight after `git init`, for the same reason. **Known duplication, deliberate.** `fixture_git_command()` now exists in NINE copies (`src/worktree.rs`, `src/app/worktrees.rs`, `src/app/api/worktrees.rs`, `src/app/api.rs`, `src/app/runtime.rs`, `src/server/notifications.rs`, `src/zynk/message.rs`, `src/workspace/git/test_support.rs`, `tests/cli_wrapper.rs`) and `seed_fixture_identity` in five, because each `#[cfg(test)]` module already carried its own near-identical `run_git`. They were hardened IN PLACE rather than folded into a new shared module: a cross-module test-support crate touches production module structure and is out of scope for a containment fix. `src/workspace/git/test_support.rs` is the one exception and is not new — it is the existing `#[cfg(test)]` module `status.rs` and `discovery.rs` already imported. Folding the nine copies together is a candidate for a later cleanup. **Tests — RED first.** Two regressions in `src/worktree.rs`, both driven the arbiter's way through an `InheritedGitDir` guard that exports `GIT_DIR` pointing at a throwaway OUTER repository with the fixture path INSIDE it. `fixture_identity_write_cannot_escape_into_a_parent_repository` calls the seeding helper under `catch_unwind` and asserts it PANICS and that the outer config never gains `zynk@example.invalid`; `fixture_repo_creation_ignores_an_inherited_git_dir` asserts `git init` still produces `<child>/.git`. Both were written against the BASE call shape and were RED in-suite on `40b6028`: `fixture_identity_write_cannot_escape_into_a_parent_repository` failed on "seeding an un-initialised fixture must abort the test, not write git config" (that assert is now `src/worktree.rs:850`) and `fixture_repo_creation_ignores_an_inherited_git_dir` on "an inherited GIT_DIR silently redirected the fixture's `git init`" (now `:872`) — 2 tests run, 0 passed, 2 failed. A shell reproduction of the same shape, run in the isolated runtime against a throwaway outer repository, showed `git init` exit 0 with no `<child>/.git`, both `git config` calls exit 0, and the outer `.git/config` gaining `[user] email = zynk@example.invalid` / `name = Zynk Test`. Every existing fixture commit is also an oracle for layer 3: with `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null` the host identity is gone, so a `git commit` in a fixture can only succeed if the `--file` write landed in the repository's own config. **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*`).** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; `-E 'test(/worktree/) + test(/workspace::git/) + test(/app::worktrees/) + test(/app::api::worktrees/)'` plus the two new cases 140/140; `--test cli_wrapper` 67 passed, 1 skipped; the full `--bin zynk` suite 2821 -> 2823 passed, 1 skipped (+2, both new, no test removed or moved); `just check` 3160 -> 3162 passed, 2 skipped, plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and 35 Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). After the full run, this checkout's own `.git/config` still carries NO `[user]` section and `git config --local --list` reports no `user.*` — the check this defect twice failed. **Invariants.** Only `#[cfg(test)]` modules and `tests/` are touched; the production git helpers are deliberately left alone (`src/worktree.rs:177` / `:293` / `:389`, `src/workspace/git/status.rs:200` / `:221`, `src/workspace/git/discovery.rs:219`, `src/zynk/message.rs::git_meta`, `src/cli/plugin.rs`), so no `AppState`/`PaneState`, detection, receipt or wire-id surface moves. `NOTICE` unchanged — this commit has no upstream counterpart. **Merge note.** The same defect is being fixed independently on the B1 branch, whose copies of these files have diverged; a later merge will conflict in them and the correct resolution is the UNION of both hardenings (scrub + containment assert + `--file` write), never a pick of one side. |
| *(this commit)* | `46174563` `f43a6d4a` `a6905364` *(all pre-relicense — no `NOTICE` entry)* | **Mouse selection becomes a setting: `ui.copy_on_select` (default `true`) turns OFF the automatic clipboard write while KEEPING the highlight, double-click still copies a word, and a config path that cannot be stat'ed stops passing for a missing one.** Three upstream commits, one fork commit, applied in upstream order because the middle one reverses the first's mechanism: `46174563` (2026-07-11) added the key and made it suppress selection entirely, `f43a6d4a` (2026-07-16, #1496 / refs #1471) took that back and redefined the key as *auto-copy* rather than *no selection*, and `a6905364` (2026-07-12) is unrelated config-read plumbing that lands last. The net tree is what this row is reviewed as; the intermediate `46174563`-only behaviour (drag suppressed, `handle_pane_double_click` early-returning on `!copy_on_select`, `reload_config` wiping the live selection when the key flips off) is NOT in the tree and must not be read back out of the upstream shas. **Config key.** `UiConfig::copy_on_select` (`src/config/model.rs:850`, default `true` at `:1046`) reaches state as the pure `bool` `AppState::copy_on_select` (`src/app/state.rs:1410`, default `true` at `:1780`), wired at construction (`src/app/mod.rs:607`) and on live reload (`:1328`). `DEFAULT_CONFIG` documents it at `src/main.rs:257-259` with `f43a6d4a`'s final wording ("Automatically copy text selected by mouse drag … double-click still copies a word"), not `46174563`'s superseded "disable mouse text selection". **Behaviour.** `AppState::handle_mouse`'s `Up(Left)` arm (`src/app/input/mouse.rs:813-834`) no longer treats "not yet copied" as "must copy": it reads `Selection::is_finalized` (renamed from `is_done` at `src/selection.rs:163` — the phase no longer implies a clipboard write, and the module lifecycle comment at `:7-8` says so), skips an already-finalized double-click selection, copies only when `copy_on_select` is set, and otherwise calls `Selection::finish()` so the highlight is retained instead of dropped. The `Down(Left)` anchor and `handle_pane_double_click` are UNCHANGED from BASE — the guards `46174563` put there were removed again by `f43a6d4a`, so an explicit double-click copies a word regardless of the setting. `App::handle_mouse` gains one hunk (`src/app/input/mod.rs:361-369`): a left press that is not itself the second click of a double-click clears a stale `selection_highlight_clear_deadline`, guarded on the selection being absent or still in progress. Without it the double-click's 1-second highlight timer survives into the NEXT drag and, now that a retained selection exists to clear, would wipe it mid-gesture; the `is_none_or(is_in_progress)` guard is what keeps a press the pane ignored (a toast click) from cancelling the deadline it should honour. **`a6905364` — read path, and what was deliberately NOT taken.** `read_optional_config` (`src/config/io.rs:69`) replaces the `path.exists()` guard in BOTH `Config::load` (`:78`) and `load_live_config` (`:180`): one read decides, `NotFound` means "no config", every other error is reported. The old guard answered `false` for a path that could not be stat'ed AT ALL — an unsearchable parent directory, a symlink loop — so a real config silently became defaults with no diagnostic. **The rest of `a6905364` is superseded by the fork's `7e292cd` (M2) and was not applied.** Upstream rewrote `config_diagnostic_summary` to collapse every diagnostic into ONE classified line (`config.toml invalid; using defaults; herdr config check`) and deleted `toml_error_location`. The fork has neither: `7e292cd` already replaced that function with one that prints the diagnostics THEMSELVES, up to four verbatim plus an "and N more" tail (`src/config/io.rs:161-178`), because full-path unknown-key reporting (`unknown config key ui.copy_on_selectt; ignoring key`) is only useful if the user can read it. Taking upstream's version would replace the fork's single source of truth for key diagnostics with a message that names no key at all. Upstream's three summary tests assert that collapsed string and were skipped for the same reason; the fork's `config_diagnostic_summary_keeps_multiple_warnings_visible` and its `reload_config_keeps_current_state_on_invalid_toml` assertion (`contains("config parse error") && contains("keeping current config")`) already encode the fork's contract and needed no edit. Upstream's `tests/cli_wrapper.rs` addition (`config_check_reports_unreadable_config_path`) was also skipped: the fork has no `zynk config check` — `run_config_command` (`src/cli.rs:250`) dispatches `reset-keys` only — so the test would not compile, and its coverage lands at the unit level instead. `tests/cli_wrapper.rs` is UNCHANGED by this commit. **Tests — RED first, 9 added, none removed.** Config key: `load_live_config_registers_copy_on_select_and_reports_misspelled_sibling` (`src/config/io.rs:1401`) in the M5-07 shape (round-trip + the misspelled sibling reported by FULL path) and `copy_on_select_default_on_and_parse` (`src/config/model.rs:1415`). Both are RED on BASE; a BASE-compilable probe of the first proved the failure mode exactly — `left: ["unknown config key ui.copy_on_select; ignoring key"], right: []`. Read path: `config_loaders_report_unreadable_path` (`:1420`) mirrors upstream and is a CHARACTERIZATION test — it passes on BASE (a directory at the config path stats fine and fails on read, `Is a directory (os error 21)`), so `config_loaders_report_an_unstattable_path_instead_of_silently_using_defaults` (`:1449`) was added to actually cover the fix: two symlinks pointing at each other make `path.exists()` return `false` while the read fails `FilesystemLoop`, and on BASE it failed on "startup must report the unreadable config" with `startup diagnostics: []` / `reload err: None`. Behaviour: five in `src/app/input/terminal.rs` — `copy_on_select_disabled_keeps_drag_selection_without_copying` (`:447`), `copy_on_select_disabled_keeps_explicit_double_click_copy` (`:489`), `new_drag_cancels_stale_double_click_highlight_deadline` (`:504`), `ignored_left_down_keeps_double_click_highlight_deadline` (`:535`) and `copy_on_select_disabled_still_forwards_mouse_reporting_gestures` (`:892`). With the two source hunks reverted in place, the first and third FAILED (`assertion failed: app.state.selection.as_ref().is_some_and(is_visible)` and `assertion failed: app.selection_highlight_clear_deadline.is_none()`) and the other three passed — they are characterization cover for what must NOT change. `reload_config_updates_live_state` (`src/app/mod.rs:2257`) gains `copy_on_select = false` in its fixture plus planted selection state (selection, autoscroll, both deadlines, `last_pane_click`) and asserts ALL of it survives the reload; with the `reload_config` assignment removed it failed on `assertion failed: !app.state.copy_on_select`. **Deliberate divergence from upstream's test edit:** `46174563` REPLACED `double_click_is_forwarded_when_mouse_reporting_is_enabled` with the new mouse-reporting test; the fork KEEPS both (`src/app/input/terminal.rs:880` and `:892`), because the old one covers double-click forwarding with `copy_on_select` at its default and the new one covers a drag with it off. Per-file `#[test]` counts: `config/io.rs` 26 -> 29, `config/model.rs` 41 -> 42, `app/input/terminal.rs` 35 -> 40; every other touched file unchanged (`app/mod.rs` 108, `app/input/mod.rs` 5, `app/input/mouse.rs` 54, `app/state.rs` 9, `selection.rs` 19, `main.rs` 5, `tests/cli_wrapper.rs` 62). **Verification (isolated `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` / `ZYNK_*` / `XDG_*`).** `cargo fmt --check` clean; `cargo clippy --all-targets --locked -- -D warnings` exits 0; `-E 'test(/selection/) | test(/config/) | test(/input::mouse/) | test(/input::terminal/) | test(/app::/)'` 915 -> 924; `--test cli_wrapper` 67 passed, 1 skipped; `-E 'test(/zynk::header/)'` 28/28; `--test zynk_receipt --test zynk_native_cli` 54/54; full `--bin zynk` 2824 -> 2832 passed, 1 skipped (2833 collected, +9, none removed); `method_wire_ids_are_stable` and `event_kind_wire_ids_are_stable` green; `just check` 3162 -> 3171 passed, 2 skipped, plus 64 maintenance unittests OK and 35 Bun asset tests; `just gate` clean (tracked-path 1695 files, scrub 82 files, gitleaks no leaks). **Invariants.** `AppState` gains one `bool` and no runtime type (no `Sender`/`Receiver`/`JoinHandle`/`Arc`/`Mutex`/`RwLock`/`Instant`/`AtomicBool`/`tokio::` in the added lines); `PaneState` untouched. `src/ui.rs` is untouched, so `render` gains no mutation. `src/app/creation.rs` (`authoritative_receiver_identity`), `src/app/api/zynk.rs`, `src/zynk/**`, `src/cli/**`, `src/cli.rs`, `src/api/**` and `src/server/headless.rs` are all untouched — no detection, receipt, identity, wire-id or `install_db_workers` surface moves. `NOTICE` unchanged: all three upstream commits predate the `cd5ea1be` (2026-07-22) relicense. |

Mode-return suppression covers the current non-popup context. **Evidence:** four original routing/loss behavioral REDs and five interface REDs precede implementation.
